This window runs about 34 hours, not the usual one. The scheduled 05:00 fetch did not run on 27-Oct or 28-Oct, most likely because the PC was off while you travelled, so fetch_announcements.py was run by hand at 05:55 IST on 28-Oct and picked up from 26-Oct 20:17. It holds 172 announcements from 91 companies. 155 PDFs downloaded, 11 failed with HTTP 403 on both links, and 6 items had no PDF. 47 companies are in COMPANIES MASTER (19 T groups) and 44 are not (8 N groups). The analysts read 1,393 text pages and 72 image-only pages, and every one of the 172 items is covered. Feed problems: the 11 failed PDFs are all SME or small listings (Urban Enviro x2, Neochem Bio x2, Karnika Industri, Macpower CNC x2, Sakar Healthcare, Unihealth Hosp, Taurian MPS, Vivid Electromech), so those cards rest on the Screener type and summary only. The 6 no-PDF items are the Asian Energy, Epigral and Sambhv Steel rating updates, two Embassy Develop PIT items and the Gallantt promoter acquisition. Many items were duplicate postings of one file: Route Mobile 7 items in one 18-page PDF; Unimech, Sky Gold and Raymond Realty 3 each; Knack, Syngene, Indiamart, Wework, L&T, Sical, Jay Bharat, Zee, Puretrop and Dynamic Cables 2 each. Five items were re-listed from the last window with no new content: Netweb and Adani Power call audio, the HFCL transcript, two United Foodbrands notices and the Bajel Lakadia award. No wrong-company file was found. Screener summaries mislabel some periods (S Chand, Neochem, Tanfac as FY26; DIC India as Q2 when it is Q3), give the wrong Share India NCD size, and put Dynamic Cables item 23 out by 10x. Prices: all 91 were refreshed from screener.in between 06:09 and 06:14 IST on 28-Oct. The PC clock had moved to UTC-4, so the script first stamped them "27-Oct 20:4x IST"; all read times were shifted by an exact +9h30 and the script now uses an IST clock. For Raymond Realty, Unimech, Syngene, L&T and Route Mobile the price is the 27-Oct close, before their results.

## Indiamart Inter.: margin jumps, but enquiries fall 14%, so exit trigger #6 fires as written

IndiaMART is in COMPANIES MASTER as HELD, "Small (2%: trimmed from 3% post Q4 FY26)", Avg Cost ₹2,015, Entry Price Max ₹1,439, MoS ₹1,151, Current Market Price ₹1,596 (Notion, properties; price dated 21-Oct in Key Notes). Promoter Verdict TRUSTWORTHY-with-flags (deepening). The page has no Downstream Signals property, so no tracker rows are linked. The page dates 27-Oct as the "next decision point". This is that print. Six items, all from 27-Oct: the board outcome and audited results (items 47, 48 and 49; 48 and 49 are byte-identical text), the press release (45), the 69-page deck (46) and the call-recording notice (44). I read every page. Three deck pages are image-only section dividers (pp.5, 59, 62) and carry no data. No call transcript is in this batch.

### What the quarter says, in numbers [FILED]

Consolidated (results, 8c89a4 p.7; deck 0a472e p.37):
- Revenue ₹428.6 Cr, up 9.6% YoY (₹391.0 Cr). Standalone ₹389.0 Cr, up 8% (8c89a4 p.15).
- EBITDA ₹159 Cr, up 23%, margin 37% against 33% a year ago (deck p.37). Standalone EBITDA ₹156 Cr, margin 40% (deck p.43).
- Other income was a LOSS of ₹7.6 Cr, against +₹106.7 Cr in Q1 (8c89a4 p.7). The fair value line on the treasury book was -₹11.1 Cr for the quarter (239c3c p.24).
- PAT ₹90.8 Cr, up 10% YoY, down 47% QoQ (8c89a4 p.7).
- CFO ₹135 Cr, up 19% YoY. H1 CFO ₹298.8 Cr (8c89a4 p.11).
- Cash and investments ₹3,234 Cr, down from ₹3,553 Cr in Q1 after the ₹360.7 Cr FY26 dividend was paid (deck p.38-39; 8c89a4 p.11).

Operating metrics, standalone (deck p.8 and p.34):
- Unique business enquiries 27M, (14%) YoY. Q2 FY26 was 31M (deck p.68).
- Active buyers (LTM) 40M, (7%) YoY. Q1 was 41M.
- Paying suppliers 217K, (2%) YoY. "615 paying suppliers declined this quarter" (deck p.8).
- Top-10% ARPU ₹365K, +12% YoY (Q1 ₹349K, +10%). Blended ARPU ₹72K, +10%.
- Employees 6,098, (6%) YoY (deck p.34).

### The gate test: the thesis-broken list, leg by leg

The page has 11 thesis-broken conditions; any one means EXIT (Notion, 4G and 8.13).

- #6, unique enquiries down more than 5% YoY for 2 consecutive quarters. Q1 FY27 was -11% printed (Notion, Q1 Step 6B item 3). Q2 FY27 is -14% printed (deck p.34 and p.68). My arithmetic on the series: 27M / 31M = -12.9%. **Both quarters are above 5% on any figure. On the page as written, #6 fires today.** The page's action tier is EXIT (Notion, 4G).
- #4, active buyers under 40M for 2 quarters. The deck prints 40M (deck p.34). On the printed figure it is not under 40M, so no strike. The deck rounds to whole millions. A -7% fall from Q2 FY26 is consistent with an unrounded figure either side of 40.0M. Depends on reading. It cannot fire this quarter either way.
- #2, paying suppliers under 209K: 217K. Pass, 8K buffer.
- #1, standalone EBITDA under 30%: 40%. Pass.
- #3 and #5, top-10% ARPU growth under 5% / 8%: +12%. Pass, and accelerating.
- #8, auditor qualification or resignation: B S R & Co. LLP, unmodified opinion on both sets (8c89a4 pp.2-6, pp.12-14). Pass.
- #9, goodwill impairment over ₹100 Cr: goodwill unchanged at ₹454.3 Cr (8c89a4 p.8). Pass.
- #7 (pledge), #10 (audit trail) and #11 (goodwill KAM): not tested by a quarterly filing. Pledge is again not stated anywhere in the deck (p.60 shows 49% promoter, no pledge line).

The other rules on the page:
- AR action tier: "net adds negative OR active buyers <40.5M OR Top 10% ARPU <8%: TRIM to 1.5%" (Notion, 8.13). Q2 meets two legs again (net -615; 40M is below 40.5M). This is the same tier already met on Q1 and not executed.
- Q1 tightened watch 8B: third straight sequential supplier fall AND active buyers under 40M means "prepare a 25% trim the following quarter" (Notion, Q1 Step 8). Leg one is met: Q4 FY26 -1,236, Q1 -1,852, Q2 -615. Leg two depends on the same 40M rounding as #4.
- 8C resolver: bull needs net adds above +2,000 and a base above 220K. Fails. Bear is "net adds below 0 again". Met, though the loss shrank from -1,852 to -615.

So the decision event resolves on the volume side the way the bear reading said it would, and the pricing and margin side came in better than the page assumed.

Your ruling on #6 has been pending since 20-Oct. Reading one: Q1 was strike one, Q2 is strike two, #6 fires and the rule says EXIT. Reading two: #6 was carried as a RED monitor in the Q1 review, not a live break, so it should not drive an exit alone. The page as written supports reading one. It now needs a decision, not a note.

The -11% versus -16% question from Q1 is now moot for #6, and the new deck mostly settles it. FY26 enquiries were 114M (deck p.68). Q2 to Q4 FY26 were 31M + 28M + 27M = 86M, which leaves about 28M for Q1 FY26 (my arithmetic, rounding about ±1.5M). Q1 FY27 at 26M against a base near 28-29M is -7% to -11%. The "-16%" reading assumed a 31M base, which is the Q2 FY26 figure. [INFERENCE] The printed -11% is the right one.

### Two readings

Bull, with evidence:
- The cost base is falling while revenue grows. Consolidated employee cost ₹171.0 Cr against ₹171.5 Cr a year ago on revenue +9.6%; headcount -6% (8c89a4 p.7; deck p.34). Standalone employee cost is 40.2% of revenue (my arithmetic, 156.2 / 389.0). That passes growth trigger #3, "Employee cost <42% of revenue" (Notion, 4C).
- Core profit is growing faster than the page models. PBT less other income, after associate losses, was ₹137.0 Cr against ₹108.4 Cr a year ago, +26% (my arithmetic, 8c89a4 p.7, same basis as the Q1 review's ₹125.0 Cr).
- Pricing still works: top-10% ARPU +9%, +10%, now +12% over three quarters.
- Supplier losses are narrowing (-1,852 to -615), and enquiries rose 26M to 27M quarter on quarter (deck p.68).
- Rolling 12-month CFO/PAT is about 1.43x (my arithmetic: FY26 CFO ₹694.2 Cr less H1 FY26 ₹275.2 Cr plus H1 FY27 ₹298.8 Cr = ₹717.8 Cr, over PAT ₹501.5 Cr on the same basis).

Bear, with evidence:
- Every demand metric fell further YoY: enquiries -11% to -14%, active buyers -5% to -7%, paying suppliers down a third straight quarter. Growth is price on a shrinking base, which is the value-trap pattern on the page (Notion, 5C).
- Business enquiries delivered are 87M, (12%) YoY (deck p.34). Fewer leads are reaching paying suppliers, and their price is still going up.
- The treasury slug reversed, as flagged. Q1 had about ₹96 Cr of unrealised fair value gain (Notion, Q1 Step 4). The deck now shows H1 "∆ in Fair Value Gain on Treasury" of ₹44 Cr (deck p.39). So Q2 gave back about ₹52 Cr (my arithmetic, not filed). Reported PAT halved quarter on quarter for that reason.
- Part of the margin gain is a cut in sales headcount. Standalone Selling and Marketing fell 3% YoY (deck p.47) while the supplier base shrinks. Cutting the sales force can protect this year's margin and weaken next year's acquisition.

The one observation that separates them: Q3 FY27 unique enquiries against the Q3 FY26 base of 28M (deck p.68), due late January 2027. At 26.6M or better (a fall within 5%), the decline is easing toward the OTP-filter share management gave. Below 26.6M is a third straight quarter worse than -5%, and the bull case loses its demand leg.

### Fair value consequence

The page's FY29 base (Notion, 3C/8.11) assumes 10% revenue CAGR and a 33% EBITDA margin. Q2 runs at +9.6% consolidated and +8% standalone, between the bear 7% and the base. Consolidated margin is 37%, above the bull case's 36%. So operating EPS is tracking at or above base on margin, while the revenue line is drifting toward bear. The base ₹78 operating EPS needs margin to hold while volume falls. That is the bet the page is now making, whether it says so or not.

Net cash per share: deck-defined cash and investments ₹3,234 Cr over 6.01 Cr shares is about ₹538 (my arithmetic; share count from deck p.60). The page's ₹602 used the AR's wider ₹3,618 Cr total treasury, which includes loans (Notion, 8.5). The definitions differ, so I do not compare them directly. At the FY26 build rate (₹2,886 Cr to ₹3,280 Cr, about ₹394 Cr a year after a ₹300 Cr dividend; deck p.38), the Year-3 base of ₹727 a share looks out of reach on the deck definition while the dividend stays at ₹361 Cr. [INFERENCE]

### What the filings do not say

- Active buyers and the YoY change in enquiries are absent from the press release. It says "27 million" with no comparison (6e6b51 p.3).
- The deck highlight page prints the declines without minus signs ("2% YoY", "7% YoY", "14% YoY", deck p.8). The full table on p.34 brackets them. This is the same framing logged as F16-01 in Q1.
- No bank-account verification percentage. This was the scoreable milestone C2 (>50% within a year) from the Q1 call (Notion, Q1 concall).
- No reason for the 6% headcount cut.
- Promoter pledge: silent for a third quarter.
- Deck p.3 still carries a "HIGHLIGHTS – Q4 & FY24-25" contents label, and the geography and ESG pages are dated 31-Mar-2026 (pp.16, 55). These are template leftovers, not flags.

### Items that close open questions

- Q1 question 1 (MonotaRO exit, no disposal line): answered. On reclassification to FVTPL, a fair value loss of ₹7.15 Cr consolidated and ₹20.0 Cr standalone was booked in other income (239c3c p.17 note 7(iv); p.47 note 7(vii); p.24 and p.53). The H1 column carries it and the Q2 column does not, so it sat inside Q1's ₹106.7 Cr. Standalone, MonotaRO cost ₹117.9 Cr is now carried at ₹58.6 Cr: ₹39.3 Cr impairment plus ₹20.0 Cr fair value loss = 50% written down (my arithmetic; the page logged 33%).
- Monitor #11 RED leg says "any single associate breaches ₹20 Cr write-down in a fiscal year" (Notion, 27-Jul). Standalone, the FY27 MonotaRO loss is exactly ₹20.0 Cr; consolidated it is ₹7.15 Cr. Depends on reading: on the consolidated figure it does not breach. Also, H1 share of associate loss is ₹29.9 Cr (8c89a4 p.7), about ₹60 Cr annualised (my arithmetic). That is above the ₹50-55 Cr AMBER band and below the ₹65 Cr RED line.
- IndiaMart Finance Ltd: incorporated 4-Aug-2026, with ₹12.05 Cr of equity, "primarily to facilitate the short-term working capital and financing requirements of business users" [FILED] (239c3c p.47 note 7(iii)). That closes the "first capitalisation" monitorable. ₹12 Cr is small, which fits management's "LSP, not own balance sheet" claim [MGMT], but the filing does not state the model.
- Results audited with a full audit report attached (8c89a4 pp.2-6, pp.12-14). That answers the 21-Oct test: quarterly audit is standing practice, now with the report.
- Signature timing: the MD/CEO signed the standalone results at 12:34 and the consolidated at 12:35 (8c89a4 p.17, p.10). The board met from 11:00 to 15:00 (p.1). That is about 2h25m before close, the second quarter running (Q1 about 2h50m, F14-01). The Audit Committee cleared the results on 26-Oct. [INFERENCE] The results item comes early in a longer meeting. A process habit, not a flag.

### Smaller items inside the statements

- Busy Infotech income tax: for AY2017-18, a ₹24.3 Cr demand over share premium received from IndiaMART. The Principal CIT rejected the stay in September 2026, a recovery notice followed, and a writ is filed in the Delhi High Court. No provision (239c3c p.32 note 31). Total income tax contingent ₹30.3 Cr. Small against ₹3,234 Cr, but it moved from dispute to recovery this half.
- A ₹1.54 Cr service tax provision was reversed after a CESTAT win on 15-May-2026 (239c3c p.22). This is immaterial to the margin.
- Busy: revenue ₹37 Cr +27%, billing ₹46 Cr +20%, EBITDA ₹4 Cr (12%, up from 9% in Q1) (deck p.52). The segment result turned positive at ₹3.2 Cr from -₹2.7 Cr in Q1 (8c89a4 p.9). New licences 11K against 12K a year ago; cumulative licences +10% (deck p.50). Against the Q1 call: C6 (revenue 27-30%) is met at the floor, while C8 (licences 15-20%) misses at 10%. Monitor #7 (billing above 25%) is not green. Overpromiser-watch stays open.
- Other investments made in H1: Fleetx ₹65.0 Cr (22.25% to 25.80%); Agillos ₹17.2 Cr advance, transferred 5-Oct to 26.94%; Livekeeping ₹6.0 Cr more in OCDs (239c3c p.17, p.47). Livekeeping already carries a ₹5.26 Cr impairment allowance in the standalone books, unchanged since 31-Mar-2026 (239c3c p.46). The page's #11 says "Livekeeping impairment ANY amount: escalating scrutiny". The allowance was on file before that rule was written and is not in the page.

### Price context

₹1,628 (screener.in, 28-Oct-2026 06:11 IST); 52-week ₹1,539 to ₹2,526; market cap ₹9,790 Cr. The 52-week low fell from ₹1,565 (21-Oct) to ₹1,539, so the stock made a new low in the week around the print. My arithmetic:
- 19.2% below your ₹2,015 cost. 2.0% above the ₹1,596 on file (21-Oct).
- 13.1% above the ₹1,439 add line. 41.4% above the ₹1,151 MoS. Inside the ₹1,439-₹2,500 HOLD band.
- Ex-cash: ₹1,628 less about ₹538 = about ₹1,090 for the operating business. On H1 core profit (₹262 Cr before tax, about ₹393 Cr a year after about 25% tax, about ₹65 a share), that is about 17x. The page's ₹53 and ₹61.5 figures give 21x and 18x. The ruling on which operating EPS is live is still pending.

The price is not near the add line, so the add-versus-exit conflict raised on 21-Oct does not arise this week.

### Verdict

Thesis-broken #6 fires on the page as written: two straight quarters of enquiries falling more than 5% (-11%, -14%). The action tier for any fired condition is EXIT. Against that, every P&L leg of the thesis is stronger than when the trigger was written: margin above the bull case, core PBT +26%, top-10% ARPU accelerating, supplier losses narrowing. The volume legs (#4, 8B) hang on whether 40M is rounded down or up. This is the moment the page's rules and its evidence point in different directions. Either honour the exit rule, or rule explicitly that #6 is a monitor and record why. Leaving it open makes the rule list decorative. Decision Status is not mine to set. That is your call.

## Route Mobile: 10% margin line cleared by ₹0.45 Cr, via add-back

Route Mobile is in COMPANIES MASTER as HELD, 4% at ₹509 average cost. The Q2 FY27 results are the print the whole page has been waiting for. Since 24-Jul every review has named one decider: consolidated Adjusted EBITDA margin. At or above 10% it retires Trigger 1. Below 10% for a second straight quarter it fires Trigger 1, and the rule on file is a mandatory 50% exit. Nine items were filed. Seven of them (items 101 to 107) are the same 18-page board outcome and results document. The other two are the press release (item 99) and the 23-page investor deck (item 100). The deck's five image-only pages are covers and section dividers, with no data on them.

### The decisive number, three ways

Management's Adjusted EBITDA for Q2 is ₹1,184.2 mn on revenue of ₹11,797 mn. That is 10.0% [FILED] (deck slide 18, p.19; slide 16, p.17). The line is 10% of ₹1,179.67 Cr, which is ₹117.97 Cr. Adjusted EBITDA is ₹118.42 Cr. So the gate clears by about ₹0.45 Cr (my arithmetic, not filed).

The same reconciliation table shows how the 10.0% was built. Reported EBITDA is ₹1,150.2 mn. Management deducts ₹35.0 mn of intangibles under development and adds back ₹69.0 mn of "professional fees related to Masivian data breach remediation" [FILED] (deck p.19). In Q1 that add-back was ₹13.6 mn. It has grown five times in one quarter. On the Q1 call management called the Masivian matter "minor, provisions cover" [MGMT] (Notion, Q1 FY27 concall addendum, prior Q6).

- Management Adjusted basis: 10.04%. Passes by 4 bps.
- Reported EBITDA, using the page's own formula (PBT 100.93 + D&A 23.84 + finance 2.46 less other income 12.21 = ₹115.02 Cr): 9.75% (my arithmetic, not filed; this matches the deck's ₹1,150.2 mn). Q1 on the same basis was logged at 9.16% (Notion, Q1 FY27 Step 1.3). On this basis it is the second straight quarter below 10%.
- Adjusted, but without the remediation add-back: (118.42 less 6.90) / 1,179.67 = 9.45% (my arithmetic, not filed).

**Gate test, Trigger 1 (EBITDA margin below 10% for 2 consecutive quarters)**
- The 24-Jul review, the 6-Aug note, the 11-Aug AR review and both 02-Sep Key Notes all define the Q2 decider as "consolidated Adjusted EBITDA margin". On that reading Trigger 1 is retired with ₹0.45 Cr to spare. The mandatory 50% exit does not fire.
- The thesis-broken list itself (Section 7 box; Section 8 item 2) says "EBITDA margin <10% for 2 consecutive Q". The Q1 review logged both "Adj 9.5% / reported ~9.16%" as below the line. On a reported reading Trigger 1 fires today: 9.16% then 9.75%.
- Depends on reading. The reported reading also has a fair point in its favour. A ₹6.9 Cr add-back is 15 times the ₹0.45 Cr headroom, and it is a cash cost the company says is not recurring. It is exactly the item that decides the gate. **Please rule on which basis Trigger 1 is tested.** On one reading nothing happens. On the other the exit rule on file is 50%.

### What improved, tested against the Q1 promise register

The Q1 call left eight dated promises on the register (Notion, Q1 FY27 concall addendum).

- "High-margin customer traffic restored" in Q2, plus the bank-customer reversal: **delivered on the evidence**. India segment revenue went from ₹236.21 Cr to ₹302.22 Cr, up 28% QoQ. India segment result swung from a ₹4.00 Cr loss to a ₹27.11 Cr profit [FILED] (results p.8). Standalone PAT doubled, ₹16.16 Cr to ₹33.13 Cr (p.14). This is the bull case's best fact.
- Gross margin in the 21.5-23% band: **delivered**. (1,179.67 less 915.72) / 1,179.67 = 22.37% (my arithmetic; the deck says 22.4%, p.17). That is up from 20.88% in Q1. On Section 8 item 1, the red leg below 22% does not repeat, so that clock resets. Cap-5% gate (GM at least 23%): **fails** again.
- Heltar close and consideration: **delivered**. Closed 29-Jul-2026. Up to ₹33.66 Cr: ₹23.88 Cr paid at closing and ₹9.78 Cr payable after 18 months [FILED] (results note 6, p.11). Notion had assumed "₹40-150 Cr". The real figure is below that range.
- Volume growth of 10-15% over "the next couple of quarters": **behind**. Billable transactions were 47.6 bn against 45.2 bn a year ago, up 5.3% YoY and up 3.9% QoQ from 45.8 bn (deck p.17; my arithmetic).
- Full-year cash conversion of 75-100% of EBITDA: **behind on both bases** (see the cash section below). H1 is 15% reported and 66% on management's adjusted figure.
- CLO/Claro firewall go-live "this quarter" (already slipped once): **silent**. Neither "Claro" nor a firewall win appears anywhere in the deck or press release. The only firewall mention is "12 Firewalls deployed" (p.5).
- Masivian attestation "very soon": **adverse**. No attestation is mentioned, and remediation fees rose from ₹1.36 Cr to ₹6.90 Cr.
- FY27 EBITDA of about 12% (Q4 FY26 guide, never withdrawn): H1 adjusted is 9.8% (deck p.18). Full-year revenue at the H1 level plus two quarters at the Q2 run-rate would be about ₹4,690 Cr. At 12% that needs about ₹563 Cr of EBITDA. Less H1's ₹227 Cr, H2 would need about 14.2% (my arithmetic, not filed). Against nine straight months below 10.5%, I read that as not credible [INFERENCE]. The CEO's press-release line that revenue was "in line with our guidance" [MGMT] (PR p.2) refers to guidance that management declined to restate on the Q1 call.

### What did not improve

- **Overseas is now the problem, not India.** Overseas segment result was ₹64.40 Cr, against ₹85.67 Cr in Q1 (down 24.8%) and ₹81.08 Cr a year ago (down 20.6%). Overseas revenue was ₹1,051.89 Cr, down 1.6% YoY [FILED] (p.8; my arithmetic). The Q1 review said overseas "now carries the entire group". The two segments swapped roles in one quarter.
- **The YoY picture is weaker than the QoQ picture.** Adjusted EBITDA fell 11.1% YoY and Adjusted PAT fell 17.8% YoY [FILED] (deck p.17). Pre-exceptional PBT fell from ₹137.87 Cr to ₹100.93 Cr, down 26.8% (my arithmetic).
- **Costs.** Employee cost was ₹90.81 Cr, up 17.0% QoQ and 26.7% YoY (p.7; my arithmetic). Headcount was 786, with 63 joiners and 60 leavers in the quarter (deck p.22). Q1 said "headcount steady ~800, wage hikes done" [MGMT]. So the ₹13.2 Cr QoQ jump is not headcount. Whether Heltar's team is inside the 786 is not disclosed. Other expenses were ₹58.12 Cr, up 47% YoY. Even excluding remediation they were up about 30%.
- **Part of the PAT recovery is tax.** The effective tax rate was 18.2% in Q2 against 25.06% in Q1 (p.7; Notion Q1 Step 1.3). At Q1's rate, Q2 PAT would be ₹75.6 Cr, not ₹82.55 Cr. About ₹6.9 Cr of the ₹14.0 Cr QoQ PAT rise is the tax line (my arithmetic, not filed). Q1 prior question 8 (tax shield exhaustion) is still unanswered.

### Cash: the vendor-advance practice is back, at ₹117 Cr

H1 cash from operations was ₹33.34 Cr, against ₹202.88 Cr in H1 FY26 [FILED] (results p.10). The swing comes from a ₹161.33 Cr increase in "financial assets and other assets". Other current assets went from ₹203.50 Cr to ₹364.23 Cr (p.9). The deck explains it: "Advance paid to vendor ₹1,168.3 mn" for "services which will be availed in the subsequent period" (deck p.20). Management adds this back to report "Adjusted CFO/EBITDA 66%" against reported 15%.

This is the practice on file. The ₹135.87 Cr FY26 exceptional was two vendor-advance write-offs. CFO Raj Gill called prepayments a "deliberate strategy for commercial benefits" on the Q4 call. The Q4 review warned that "further periodic write-offs [are] structurally likely" (Notion, Q4 FY26 Step 0D and Section B). The Q4 FY26 watchpoint 5 was "Any new large vendor prepayments disclosed". The answer is yes: ₹116.8 Cr, which is 86% of the size of the advances written off last year.

- Monitorable 3 (trailing-4Q CFO/PAT at least 1.0x): H2 FY26 CFO ₹378.28 Cr (FY26 ₹581.16 Cr per the Notion AR review, less H1 FY26 ₹202.88 Cr) plus H1 FY27 ₹33.34 Cr = ₹411.62 Cr. Trailing PAT is ₹216.99 Cr plus ₹151.10 Cr = ₹368.09 Cr. That gives 1.12x, so **green** (my arithmetic, not filed). On H1 alone it is 0.22x.
- The promise of 75-100% of EBITDA: fails at 15% reported. It also fails at 66% even after management's own add-back.
- Payable days fell from 64 to 50 while DSO went from 76 to 72 (deck p.21). The cash cycle widened from about 12 to about 22 days (my arithmetic). Paying suppliers faster and paying them in advance are the same direction.
- Standalone receivables rose from ₹241.36 Cr to ₹391.25 Cr, up 62% in six months. Standalone CFO was negative ₹126.69 Cr and standalone cash fell from ₹247.03 Cr to ₹73.99 Cr (p.15-16). On H1 standalone revenue of ₹454.26 Cr, that is roughly 157 receivable days (my arithmetic). The India recovery came with collections that have not arrived yet. The AR's Customer A (15.1% of revenue) is the obvious candidate. That is inference only.
- Monitorable 10 (net cash at least ₹800 Cr): **green**. Cash is ₹13,556 mn (deck p.4) and borrowings are nil (p.9). That is down about ₹33 Cr from ₹1,388.67 Cr at FY26 after ₹40.63 Cr of dividends and ₹23.88 Cr for Heltar.

**Bull reading of the advance:** it buys route capacity at a better price. That is the "enhanced routing strategy" the deck credits for the GM recovery (p.17) [MGMT]. GM did rise 150 bps.
**Bear reading:** the same mechanism produced ₹136 Cr of write-offs within two years. Now a single advance is equal to roughly a full year of H1 PAT, and it is being added back in the cash KPI.
**The separating observation:** other current assets at 31-Mar-2027 (Q4 balance sheet). Back below about ₹250 Cr means the services were consumed. Above ₹364 Cr means the advance is rolling or growing.

### Other monitorables touched

- 5, New Products YoY at least 15%: **green**. ₹1,284 mn, up 36.7% YoY and 35.8% QoQ. Mix is 10.9%, up from 8.2% in Q1 (deck p.10; my arithmetic). The deck does not split Heltar's two months out of the QoQ jump, so the organic rate is not knowable.
- 6, Top-10 concentration at most 45%: **green** at 42% (deck p.13). The single-customer red line above 18% cannot be tested, because single-customer data is not in the deck. Clients above $1 mn (annualised) fell from 80 to 73, and above $10 mn from 9 to 8 (p.13).
- 13, Truecaller BM KPI: the word "Truecaller" appears nowhere in the press release or the deck. The green test was "disclosed by Q2 FY27, ₹15+ Cr/qtr". **That deadline is missed.** On the red test ("3 quarters silent"), the clock reads 3 of 3 if the AR counts, as the 11-Aug review counted it. It reads 2 of 3 if only quarterly prints count. **Please rule.**
- 14, Heltar: consideration disclosed; "Development led by the Heltar core team" (deck p.9) implies the founders are retained; no ₹ revenue attribution. By the page's own definition that is **AMBER**. The "₹5+ Cr/qtr by Q2 FY27" green test fails.
- 15, FY27 M&A pace under ₹300 Cr: **green**, ₹33.66 Cr cumulative.
- 16, Salesforce AgentExchange: named in the deck (p.7), no revenue. Silence clock 1 of 4 continues.
- Strategic Premium expiry (+1.25x to +1.0x unless the "Q2 FY27 concall" gives one Proximus revenue KPI): the filings give none. The analyst call is on 28-Oct (deck cover letter p.1). The test is still pending until the transcript arrives. If the call is also silent, destination PE drops by 0.25x.

### Board items besides results

- **IPO money rescission [FILED] (outcome p.2).** On 7-May-2026 the board approved a variation of the unspent ₹65 Cr IPO "purchase of office premises in Mumbai" object. It has now rescinded that variation, so the ₹65 Cr stays with the office purchase. The IPO was in 2020, so the money has been idle for six years (p.11 note 3). Neither filing states what the variation was or why it was rescinded. Q1 prior question 9 asked about exactly this and is "not addressed" on file. Notion never logged the 7-May variation itself. One reason is possible [INFERENCE]. Varying IPO objects needs a special resolution, plus an exit offer from the promoter to shareholders who vote against (Companies Act s.27). The 02-Sep AGM notice carried zero special resolutions (Notion, AR review Step 8). Going back to the 2020 object avoids both. It also leaves ₹65 Cr earmarked for a building purchase that has not happened in six years.
- **MOA alteration (outcome p.18).** New main objects for AI, GenAI, ML, digital identity, cloud and CPaaS. It needs shareholder approval. "Digital identity" is Telesign's business inside Proximus Global (PR p.3). The bull reading: this enables Proximus-stack products to be sold through Route, which is the synergy the Strategic Premium needs. The bear reading: it widens the scope for related-party flows with the parent, on top of the new ₹10.11 Cr Proximus management-fee RPT from FY26 (Notion, AR review). Watch the postal-ballot notice for any RPT cap.
- ESOP lapse of 2,860 options, against 22,000 in Q1. Immaterial.
- Second interim dividend ₹4, record date 2-Nov. H1 total is ₹8, on track for the ₹16.5 "sustainable" claim if two more ₹4 interims follow.

### Audit scope and small inconsistencies

- This is the first MSKA review (p.3-5). Twenty-four subsidiaries were reviewed by other auditors. Their Q2 PAT was ₹65.43 Cr before consolidation adjustments (p.4). That is 79% of the ₹82.55 Cr group PAT. On the same basis Q1 was 35.3%: H1 ₹89.62 Cr less Q2 ₹65.43 Cr = ₹24.19 Cr, over ₹68.55 Cr (my arithmetic; this matches the Notion Q1 finding F4). The share relying on component auditors more than doubled in one quarter. Q1 prior question 10 asked about bringing these under principal review and was "dodged".
- Standalone note 6 labels the Q1 column "30.06.2025". The ₹4.22 Cr subsidiary dividend is a Q1 FY27 figure, so this is a typo (p.17). Minor.
- The deck is dated 27-Oct (p.2), but the cover letter says it will be presented on 28-Oct (p.1). The press release headline compares PAT against a ₹18.83 Cr loss a year ago (PR p.2). That comparison is flattered by last year's exceptional item. The release does print pre-exceptional PBT alongside it. The board met in Brussels from 9:00 to 10:35 PM IST (p.2, p.11).

### Price, and two rules that fire together

₹448 (screener.in, 28-Oct-2026 06:13 IST). The 52-week range is ₹407-771 and market cap is ₹2,824 Cr. This read was taken after the 11:49 PM filing but before any trading on it. It is the 27-Oct close, before the results reaction.

- **Hard floor ₹450** (Position Size property; Q4 FY26 Step 8, "raised from ₹420"). Price is ₹2 below it. The action on file for a floor breach is "Exit 50% regardless of narrative" (Devil's Advocate implication 4; RDE pre-defined exit criteria).
- **Add rule** (Q1 FY27 Step 8): add only if price is at most ₹510 AND Q2 Adjusted EBITDA margin is at least 10%. On management's basis, both legs are met today.
- So the same ₹448 meets the add rule and breaches the floor. **Please rule on which governs.** One reading treats the floor as a hard price stop that the add rule cannot override. The other treats the floor as a reference to the cash value per share, which a passed margin gate supersedes.
- Other lines on file: ₹448 is 12.0% below the ₹509 average cost. It is below the ₹502 MoS property and below the ₹463 MoS set in the 11-Aug AR review body. The page properties still show Entry ₹628 / MoS ₹502, while the body's latest values are ₹579 / ₹463. The properties were never updated. Net cash of about ₹1,356 Cr is 48% of market cap, about ₹215 per share. Trailing EPS is ₹55.43 (H1 FY27 ₹22.57 plus H2 FY26 ₹32.86), so the stock trades at 8.1x (my arithmetic, not filed).

### Fair value consequence

Revenue is running at about +6.4% for FY27 (₹4,690 Cr against ₹4,408 Cr; my arithmetic). That is below the revised 9-10% Base and above the ₹4,648 Cr Bear FY27E. Margins are running at Bear: GM 22.4% against a Bear FY29 of 22% and a Base FY27E of 23.5%, and the management band tops out at 23%. Adjusted EBITDA is 9.8% for H1 against Bear FY29 at 11% and Base FY27E at 12.5%. The ₹1,131 PW fair value carries 35% weight on a Base case that needs GM above management's own ceiling. Nothing in this print supports that leg. The Growth Premium (+3.5x) and the Strategic Premium (+1.25x, expiry test pending) both look exposed. I would not cut either before the concall.

### Verdict in your frame

- Trigger 1: retired on the Adjusted basis, fired on the reported basis. That needs your ruling.
- Triggers 2 to 5 (Proximus review, digital-native losses, ROCE, arbitration plus a second dispute): not touched by these filings.
- The hard floor is breached by ₹2 on a pre-results print, and it conflicts with the add rule.
- The deferred RDE 4% to 3% trim is still with you.
- On the evidence, the name is no stronger than it was on 24-Jul. Margin got back to the line through an add-back. Overseas profit fell by a quarter. Cash conversion is 15%. The vendor-advance practice that cost ₹136 Cr is running again at ₹117 Cr.
- The real improvement is India: it recovered as promised. Heltar was small and is now disclosed. New Products is up 37%.
- I read this as moving toward the weakened end, not toward an add. That is your call.

## KRN Heat Exchanger: the call you expected to miss is on

Item 53. KRN will hold a Q2/H1 FY27 earnings call on Friday 30-Oct-2026 at 3:00 PM IST, the day after the 29-Oct board meeting. Dolat Capital is hosting [FILED] (KRN_Heat_Exchan_Announcement_under_Regulation_30_LODR-An_72b295.txt, p.1-2). Three people will speak for the company: CMD Santosh Kumar Yadav, CFO Pawan Nawal, and Ms. Komal Yadav, Whole Time Director of the subsidiary KRN HVAC Products (p.2). The letter mentions no presentation.

This overturns the main point of the 22/23-Oct review. That review read the 21-Oct intimation's silence as "likely no call". It then built two of its three pending rulings on that reading (Notion, Board Intimation Review 21-Oct, Rulings pending):
- Does a second quarter without a call move Promoter Verdict MONITOR toward CONCERN? This is now moot, if the call takes place.
- Does "utilisation undisclosed" alone meet the Q2 bear branch of 8C? This is no longer something to rule on in advance. It becomes a test on the call. If analysts ask for Neemrana utilisation and management does not give a number, the bear leg is met on the call record itself. That is a stronger basis than a missing call.
- The third ruling, whether ₹930 is a fixed or a trailing stop, is still open and not affected.

Who is on the call matters more than the call itself. Notion lists Komal Yadav in the promoter group (Notion, AR deep dive, Section E). The Q4 FY26 call had a "Ms. KL (Director KRN HVAC Pvt Ltd subsidiary)" (Notion, Q4 FY26 Concall Analysis). This is probably the same person, now named with a WTD title [INFERENCE]. She runs the entity behind the open governance questions:
- Q14 (HIGH): subsidiary PAT ₹6.11 Cr for all of FY26 against ₹14.23 Cr in Q1 FY27 alone. Is intra-group pricing being shifted? (Notion, AR review, Q14)
- Q3 and Q17: the subsidiary's numbers (43.2% of Q1 group PAT) were not reviewed by the principal auditor, and the subsidiary auditor is not named in the AR.
- Q1: the ₹24.5 Cr intra-group stock-in-trade in Q1, and the ₹211.66 Cr FY26 trading line.
- Q2: the ₹143.18 Cr of QIP money sitting idle in FDs and bonds, held in the subsidiary.

If the subsidiary's own director attends and these still get no numbers, it is harder to call that an honest gap. It looks more like a choice [INFERENCE].

What the 29/30-Oct pair can now test, leg by leg (thresholds from Notion, Q1 FY27 review and 21-Oct review):
- Checklist red #1, Neemrana utilisation below 50% at Q2 FY27: testable on the call. The FY26 figure was 15%, and FY27's 50% target was reaffirmed on the Q4 call (Notion, Q4 FY26 Concall, Step 3E).
- 8C cash test: H1 consolidated CFO/PAT above 0.80x with utilisation at 50% or more is the bull branch. Below 0.30x, or utilisation below 35% or undisclosed, is the bear branch. The results give the cash number. The call gives utilisation.
- Thesis-broken (i) data-centre share below 20% by FY29 and (ii) utilisation below 40% by FY28: both were "UNTESTABLE this doctype" in Q1. Both are testable again on 30-Oct. Q4 FY26 DC share was 19% (Notion, Section 13).
- Soft trim: still cannot fire on H1. Leg (a) needs FY27 revenue growth below 30%. Q1 was +118.9% (Notion, Q1 FY27 review).
- Daikin (Q11): a fifth straight non-mention is the logged escalation trigger (Notion, Section 13 watchpoints).

The one observation that separates the readings is H1 CFO/PAT together with a stated utilisation figure, on 29-30 Oct. Above 0.80x and at least 50% supports the bull case. Below 0.30x, or utilisation dodged on a call where the subsidiary director is present, supports the bear case.

Small items in the filing:
- The letter says "Q2H1&FY27" in the body and "Q2H1FY27" in the subject. The invite on p.2 says "Q2 & H1 FY27". This is drafting sloppiness, the same kind of drafting-control issue logged as F14 in Q1. It is not a flag.
- No company secretary or IR head is listed among the speakers. The Q4 call had the CS on it (Notion, Q4 FY26 Concall).

Price. ₹1,123 (screener.in, 28-Oct-2026 06:11 IST), 52-week range ₹590 to ₹1,687, market cap ₹7,350 Cr. My arithmetic, not filed:
- 20.8% above the ₹930 stop. 42.2% above the ₹790 cost.
- 33.4% below the 52-week high. 5.9% above the ₹1,060 QIP price. About level with the promoters' ₹1,117 block sale.
- 3.7x the ₹300 add line. 91x FY26 EPS of ₹12.30. Up 6.4% since ₹1,055 on file (22-Oct).
- Market cap divided by price gives 6.55 Cr shares, which matches the 6.546 Cr after the QIP. There is still no visible ESOP dilution.

No trigger fires. HELD is unchanged. The call removes the main excuse for the record to stay unresolved. That is your call.

## Vivid Electromech: deck moves Ambernath to November, gate 3 fails

Vivid is in COMPANIES MASTER as WATCHLIST, not held. The Position Size field reads "STARTER (1%) at ₹1,200: conditional on Q1 FY27 print validation; AVOID at CMP ₹1,368" (Notion, properties). Ideal entry ₹877 and MoS ₹700 (Notion, 11-Sep Key Notes), with an indicative entry of about ₹822 and MoS about ₹575 pending the Role 1 rerun (Notion, 24-Oct H1 review, Step 7). The Entry Price Max and Margin of Safety Price properties are blank. Six tracker rows are linked; I read all six.

Source limit, stated first. The PDF did not download (HTTP 403 twice, INDEX.md row 164). There is no text file and no page image. Everything below rests on the Screener summary of the 26-Oct investor presentation: "H1 FY27 revenue rose 29.7% to ₹91.53 crore; order book ₹307 crore and factory commissioning targeted Nov 2026." I treat those three figures as [MGMT, via Screener summary], not as read from the deck. The deck itself would add: the date and definition of the ₹307 Cr book, its DC / metro / other split, the exact Ambernath wording (trial runs, commissioning or commercial operation), whether the 35-40% FY27 guide stands, and any receivables or intangible explanation.

### 1. Ambernath: a third date, and the gate as written fails

Dates on file for Ambernath:
- End-August 2026 (AR, 27-Jul) (Notion, 25-Sep AR review).
- End-October 2026 (deck, 5-Sep, p.13) (Notion, 25-Sep AR review).
- Now "targeted Nov 2026" (deck, 26-Oct, per Screener summary).

That is the third date in three months. The AR also called the facility "commissioned" in July (Notion, 25-Sep, disclosure-quality points). The slips are about a month each.

Gate test, leg by leg (Role 3 two-of-three rule; Notion, 24-Oct Step 6C):
- Gate 1, receivables below ₹100 Cr: failed on 23-Oct at ₹119.75 Cr. Untouched.
- Gate 2, margin above 19%: passed at 20.08%. Untouched.
- Gate 3, Ambernath on track for October: the tracker row's falsifier is "No Ambernath commercial-operations filing with NSE by 31-Oct-2026: AMBER, and the second failed gate under the Role 3 two-of-three rule ... so AVOID is proposed" (tracker row, Ambernath commercial operations filing). Management's own deck now puts commissioning in November. Unless an NSE COD filing lands in the next three days, against the company's own target, gate 3 fails on 31-Oct as written.

So the two-of-three rule is about to fire, and on the company's own timeline. The 24-Oct review pre-committed the consequence: "if no Ambernath COD is filed by 31-Oct-2026, two of three Role 3 gates have failed. Propose AVOID, with re-entry on (a) COD filed and (b) Mar-27 receivables 110 or less" (Notion, Step 8).

Monitoring items:
- #26 Ambernath COD specificity: GREEN if filed by 31-Oct, AMBER Nov-Dec 2026, RED past Jan-27 (Notion, 14-Jul entry). A November target moves it to AMBER.
- #4 Ambernath commissioning: green "Phase 1 by 31-Oct-26", red "slip beyond Jan-27". AMBER on the same reading.
- The hard thesis-broken line (Ambernath past 31-Jan-2027) does not fire. November is two months inside it.

Ruling needed. Does gate 3 fail on 31-Oct as written, which makes AVOID the proposal under your own rule? Or do you re-point the gate to a November filing because the company has now named a month? My read: it fails as written. The gate was set as a date, and the company set and missed the date itself. Re-pointing every time the target moves would turn the gate into a moving target. This sits alongside the open 25-Oct ruling on whether a call statement counts. The call is today, 28-Oct, at noon (Notion, 25-Oct entry). A "commissioned" claim on the call would be [MGMT] against a deck that says November.

### 2. The ₹307 Cr order book: demand is not the constraint

On file: ₹210 Cr at 30-Jun (AR, includes Univastu) and ₹205 Cr at 31-Aug (deck: DC ₹98 Cr, Metro ₹32 Cr, Others ₹75 Cr) (Notion, 25-Sep correction 4). The 26-Oct review set the bull marker as "book at or above ₹250 Cr ex-GST" (Notion, 26-Oct entry).

My arithmetic, not filed:
- ₹307 Cr less ₹205 Cr = ₹102 Cr, up about 50% from 31-Aug.
- Filed October inflow is ₹76.13 Cr (₹35.77 Cr on 23-Oct plus ₹40.36 Cr on 26-Oct) (Notion, Key Notes 26-Oct). ₹205 + ₹76.13 = ₹281 Cr before any September-October execution.
- Q2 revenue was ₹58.85 Cr (Notion, Step 1), so September alone probably shipped ₹15-25 Cr [INFERENCE]. To reach ₹307 Cr, roughly ₹45-50 Cr of orders must have come in that are not in any Reg 30 filing on file, or the book definition is gross of GST or installation scope.
- If the ₹307 Cr is dated 30-Sep, it cannot include the October orders, and the unfiled gap is larger still, about ₹120 Cr after September execution.

So the number clears the ₹250 Cr bull marker on its face. It cannot yet be reconciled with filed orders. The date and the GST basis decide whether it is real growth or a definition change. Item #5 of the IR questions (Notion, Step 8.5) asked exactly this: "does it include GST or unexecuted installation scope?" The deck answers the size and leaves the basis open.

Book against the run-rate (my arithmetic, not filed): ₹307 Cr / TTM revenue ₹221.10 Cr (Notion, Step 6.5A) = 1.39x, against 205 / 221 = 0.93x logged as "no" in the Pillar 3a test (Notion, Step 7). That leg may flip at the Role 1 rerun. The threshold for 3a is not on the page, so I cannot say it passes.

The real point is what the two items do together. The H2 FY27 bar is ₹168 Cr implied at FY26 seasonality, ₹178-188 Cr on the guide, bull ₹170 Cr or more, bear ₹145 Cr or less (Notion, Steps 3 and 8C). A ₹307 Cr book covers any of those. The existing plants ran at about 94% utilisation and produced ₹129.57 Cr in H2 FY26 (Notion, Step 3). So the constraint is capacity, not orders. That fits the 5.5B reading already on file: "a timing or capacity issue at the target, demand intact" (Notion, Step 5.5B). Each month Ambernath slips takes shipping capacity out of a half that already needs +30-45%.

### 3. H1 revenue figure: consistent with the filing

₹91.53 Cr, +29.7% YoY, matches the 23-Oct results exactly (Notion, Step 1 and Step 2). No restatement. The deck repeats the number that sits below the 35-40% guide. The Screener summary does not say whether the guide is retained. If the deck holds 35-40% with H1 at 29.7% and the plant a month later, H2 must grow 38-45% (Notion, Step 3) on capacity that is not live yet.

### Two readings

Bull, with evidence:
- The book at ₹307 Cr is about 50% above 31-Aug and above the ₹250 Cr marker. October DC orders (STT, ABS Chennai) are on file.
- A one-month slip still leaves four to five months of H2 for Ambernath output.
- The company published a deck two days before its first public call. The 25-Oct entry noted "No investor presentation is mentioned" (Notion, 25-Oct). That gap is now closed, and the 10-Sep meet had none.

Bear, with evidence:
- Third Ambernath date. The capex money is in FDs to April 2027: ₹11.88 Cr (tracker row, Reg 32 Annexure B).
- The book does not reconcile with filed orders by about ₹45-50 Cr or more.
- H2 needs the plant. Without it, H2 near ₹145 Cr is the bear marker "Ambernath added nothing" (Notion, Step 8C).
- Gate 3 fails on the date, so the two-of-three rule fires.

The one observation that separates them: an NSE commercial-operations filing for Ambernath by 30-Nov-2026, with the date and GST basis of the ₹307 Cr book given on today's call. COD filed in November plus a dated, ex-GST book of at least ₹250 Cr supports the bull path at the H2 print (₹170 Cr or more by about 30-May-2027). No COD by 31-Dec moves #26 to the edge of RED and puts the 31-Jan-2027 thesis-broken line in play.

### Fair value

No projection change from a summary line. The 24-Oct indicative path already trimmed FY27 revenue to about ₹259 Cr and FY30 EPS to about ₹73, with Ambernath "slips about 2 months" (Notion, Step 7). A November COD is about one month beyond end-October, so it fits inside that slip. The larger book supports the revenue line only if capacity ships it. Fair value stays tied to the Role 1 rerun.

### Price

₹1,253 (screener.in, 28-Oct-2026 06:14 IST). 52-week ₹556 to ₹1,990, market cap ₹1,114 Cr. My arithmetic:
- Up 8.1% from ₹1,159 on file (screener.in, 26-Oct 20:31 IST, Key Notes). The stock went back above the starter line the day after the orders and deck.
- 4.4% above the ₹1,200 starter, 3.6% below the Role 3 avoid-above-₹1,300 line.
- 43% above the ₹877 ideal entry and 52% above the indicative ₹822.
- 31.6x TTM operating EPS of ₹39.65 (Notion, Step 6.5A).

The starter is not armed at any price. Gate 1 failed, gate 3 is about to fail, and the 27-Jul KMP condition is pending (Notion, Step 8). The Position Size text still says "AVOID at CMP ₹1,368", three months stale.

### What it does not say (from the summary)

- The date of the ₹307 Cr book, its GST basis, and its split.
- Whether "commissioning" means commercial operation.
- Whether the FY27 guide stands.
- Anything on the ₹10.98 Cr intangible, the ₹9.68 Cr advances or receivables ageing (tracker row, intangible and advances).

Verdict: the Role 2 / Role 3 hard thesis-broken lines do not fire. The Role 3 two-of-three rule is set to fire on 31-Oct on management's own timeline. On your pre-commitment that points to AVOID, with re-entry on a filed COD and Mar-27 receivables at ₹110 Cr or less. The order book says demand is fine. The thesis was always about the plant. That is your call.

## Bandhan Bank: second Gulf rumour denied as price nears stop

Bandhan Bank is in COMPANIES MASTER as WATCHLIST / AVOID. Zero position held. Entry Price Max ₹199, calibrated 25% band ₹170 to ₹180, stop ₹150, MoS ₹135. Promoter Verdict AVOID (Notion, properties; Key Notes 29-Jun PM). Three tracker rows are linked and I read all three.

Item 15 is a one-page Reg 30(11) clarification, read in full [FILED] (Bandhan_Bank_Rumour_verification_-_Regulation_3011_a98c15.pdf, p.1):
- Moneycontrol on 27-Oct-2026 ran "First Abu Dhabi Bank scouts for acquisition in India". Bandhan is "one of the banks named in the article".

- Ref BBL/SEC/188/2026-27, signed by the Company Secretary at 13:02 IST the same day.

Tested against the record:
- The wording is stronger than the 13-Oct IHC reply. That one said "not aware" and "not received any communication" (Notion, 14-Oct entry). This one is an active denial of discussions. That is a real difference.
- The gap on file is the same. The bank speaks for itself. A stake sale would be negotiated at the promoter holding company, and the CEO said in April that CIC-level activity "is their call" (Notion, DA Section 6). The promoter entity has filed nothing in either case [INFERENCE that it would need to].

- Unlike the 13-Oct letter, this one gives no price move.
- DA early warning "Promoter Entity Activity: any ownership change announcement at promoter level" (Notion, DA Section 8): not fired. A report plus a denial is not an announcement.
- Tracker row "Promoter-level ownership change": its test is any promoter-entity filing or RBI-application filing by 12-Nov-2026. Still open. Today adds a second Gulf name in 14 days to the observation, and no filing.
- Record gap: 13-Oct was BBL/SEC/180. Today is /188. Refs 181 to 187 are not in the record, on top of 175 to 179. One of these is probably the Q2 board intimation [INFERENCE]. The Q2 results date is NOT FOUND in the record.

- ₹24,475 Cr ÷ ₹152 = 161.0 Cr shares, which ties to the 161.1 Cr on file.
- Down 11.6% from ₹172 on file (14-Oct). 3.4% below the ₹157.35 pre-IHC-rumour price. The rumour premium is gone, and then some.
- 1.3% above the ₹150 stop. 12.6% above the ₹135 MoS. 23.6% below the ₹199 entry max.
- Below the ₹170 to ₹180 calibrated band, below the ₹164 "BUY: CMP below ₹164" line on the original verdict card, and below the ₹160 to ₹167 Tranche 3 zone.
- About 0.96x FY26 book of ₹159.

Gate test on the re-entry conditions, leg by leg:
- Permanent CFO with comparable-bank credentials: fails. Vinay Jain is interim to 31-Mar-2027 (Notion, Key Notes 04-Oct, 09-Oct).
- New CFO restating FY27 ROA, NIM and credit cost bands: fails. No permanent CFO.
- CASA thesis-broken condition: level leg failed at 26.71% (30-Sep). The "sustained" ruling is still pending (ruling 32).

Two readings:
- Bull: the stock is below book for a bank guiding ROA of 1.2 to 1.4% at the Q4 FY27 exit (Notion, Q1 review). Two denials in two weeks remove rumour noise from the price, so what is left is fundamental. The FAB denial is categorical.
- Bear: the 11.6% fall came with no new filed fundamental in the record. It follows the CASA break, and the market is pricing the Q2 print before it lands. Two foreign-acquirer stories in 14 days, both denied only at bank level, keep the promoter question open.

EEB 0-90 DPD above Q1's 3.5% confirms the forward-flow worry (Notion, 22-Jul). Cost of funds within guidance and DPD at or below 3.5% supports the bull reading. Then the 12-Nov promoter-filing test.

Cross-links: the deposit-mix row names AU, Ujjivan and Equitas Q2 CASA as the peer check. None is in this window's file.

Verdict: the filing itself is low value; it denies a weak rumour. No thesis-broken condition fires and WATCHLIST / AVOID stands. But the price has moved through every buy line on the card while every gate still refuses. Ruling 31, a rule for "price invites, gates refuse", was asked at ₹178 and ₹172. It is now live at ₹152, one step from the stop. That is your call.

## Syngene Intl.: sequential beat, but H1 cash went negative

Syngene is in COMPANIES MASTER under a SHALLOW WATCH posture. Decision Status is blank, with an operator ruling pending (Notion, Key Notes 28-Sep). There is no Entry or MoS. Indicative value is about ₹150-225, and the size ceiling is Small, 2-3% (Notion, Section 6 and Section 8). I read all 15 linked tracker rows.

Five items, one event: the 27-Oct board meeting. Items 139 (results) and 140 (board outcome) are the same 11-page file. I read every page of all four documents, plus the six image pages of the deck. All six are section-divider slides with no numbers. The call is at 9:00 IST today, after this run. So everything below is the filings only.

What the quarter says [FILED] (consolidated, Results p.2; fact sheet p.8; deck p.26):

- Revenue from operations ₹832.7 Cr, +13% QoQ (Q1 ₹736.0 Cr), -8.6% YoY (Q2 FY26 ₹910.6 Cr).
- Operating EBITDA ₹147.5 Cr, margin 17.7%, against 12.3% in Q1 and 21.9% a year ago.
- Foreign exchange loss ₹61.4 Cr in the quarter. It was ₹50.1 Cr in Q1, so H1 is ₹111.5 Cr (Results p.2, row g).
- PAT before exceptional items ₹37.7 Cr. After exceptional items it is ₹32.2 Cr. H1 PAT before exceptional items is ₹38.8 Cr.
- Exceptional termination benefits again: ₹6.7 Cr pre-tax (Results p.8, note 7). That makes four quarters running.
- H1 operating cash flow is **negative ₹143.2 Cr** (Results p.4).

### T3 fires as written, and the tripwire needs a ruling

The row says T3 fires if "H1 FY27 CFO below 70% of operating EBITDA" (Tracker, T3 row).

- H1 operating EBITDA ₹238.2 Cr (fact sheet p.7). H1 CFO is -₹143.2 Cr (Results p.4). That is -60% of EBITDA. My arithmetic, not filed. The test is 70%. It fails on any reading of the words.
- What drove it [FILED, Results pp.3-4]: liabilities fell by ₹382.2 Cr. Other current liabilities alone went from ₹780.8 Cr to ₹467.4 Cr, down ₹313.4 Cr. Receivables released ₹116.9 Cr. Inventory absorbed ₹26.5 Cr. Taxes paid were ₹52.5 Cr.
- Net cash fell from ₹1,800 Cr (31-Mar, deck p.27) to ₹1,392 Cr (30-Sep, deck p.26), down ₹408 Cr in a half. Notion logs ₹1,541 Cr at June [MGMT] (Notion, V4). So Q2 alone used about ₹149 Cr. Short-term borrowings went from ₹9.4 Cr to ₹67.2 Cr (Results p.3). My arithmetic, not filed.
- The bridge ties: CFO -₹143 Cr, capex ₹220 Cr, dividend ₹50.5 Cr, leases ₹27.5 Cr come to about -₹441 Cr. Interest received of ₹22 Cr and FX on cash bring that close to the ₹408 Cr fall (Results p.4). My arithmetic, not filed.

The bull reading has evidence. H1 FY26 CFO was also negative, at -₹45.1 Cr against ₹405.9 Cr of operating EBITDA (Results p.4; fact sheet p.7). That is -11%, which would also have failed this test. Yet FY26 full-year CFO was validated by the net-cash bridge (Notion, V4; Tracker, T3 row). Notion also logged a "seasonal advance unwind" at June (Notion, V4). So some of this is a first-half pattern, not a new problem.

The bear reading has the same weight. H1 is ₹98 Cr worse than last year, on ₹168 Cr less EBITDA. The liabilities outflow is ₹382 Cr against ₹266 Cr last year (Results p.4). The ₹313 Cr fall in other current liabilities most likely means customer advances being used up and not refilled [INFERENCE]. That is the Zoetis pattern: advances were paid when Zoetis was buying and are not replaced now that it is not. The filing does not split the line.

Please rule. Either T3 fires as written, or T3 was set without allowing for H1 seasonality and should be restated. One restatement would be H1 CFO against the prior-year H1. The other would test full-year FY27 CFO against 70% of operating EBITDA. On the YoY reading it still fails (-₹143 Cr against -₹45 Cr).

### R2/T1 cannot be tested from the filings, and the silence matters

Research revenue is management's stated research share times revenue (Notion, Section 1; Tracker, R2/T1 row). The deck (pp.5-28), press release and results do not give the research or CDMO split. The results say the group "operates in a single segment" (Results p.8, note 9). So the split will come only on today's call.

What the call has to show, on Q2 revenue of ₹832.7 Cr (my arithmetic, not filed):

- R2 Q2 leg (research ≥ ₹610 Cr) passes only if the research share is 73.2% or more.
- T1 (research < ₹580 Cr) fires if the share is below 69.6%.
- Between those two, neither fires.
- Q1 was 78% (Notion, Key Notes 09-Oct). Q2 FY26 was about 67% (Notion, V3: ₹610 Cr of ₹910.6 Cr).

One pointer. The press release credits "positive traction in our CDMO business" for the 13% sequential growth (press release p.2) [MGMT]. It does not credit research. Suppose research only held at Q1's ₹574 Cr. Then CDMO was about ₹259 Cr, up about 60% QoQ, and the research share was 68.9%. On that reading T1 fires. That is my arithmetic, and the reading is inference. The ₹97 Cr sequential gain went somewhere. Management chose to name CDMO.

### R1: the first Bayview contract does not meet the gate

What it says [MGMT, press release p.3; deck p.5]: Syngene "signed its first manufacturing agreement for the Bayview biologics facility with a large pharmaceutical customer". Process development starts in India this fiscal year. Drug substance manufacturing at Bayview is "expected to begin in the next financial year", which is FY28. Separately, Unit 3 got "an agreement for clinical supplies".

R1 needs a NAMED commercial-scale customer, or a PPQ campaign, or a disclosed utilisation figure, at Unit 3 or Bayview, by 31-Mar-2027 (Tracker, R1 row). Trial batches do not count.

- Named: fail. "A large pharmaceutical customer".
- Commercial scale or PPQ: not stated. "Drug substance manufacturing" could be clinical or commercial.
- Utilisation: not disclosed.
- Unit 3: clinical supplies, which the row excludes.

So R1 does not fire. But this is the first Bayview contract of any kind, after four slipped start dates (Notion, V1). It is probably one of the "one or two" project conversions the chair mentioned in July (Notion, V1) [INFERENCE]. What is missing: the customer, the value, the molecule stage, the batch count, and the term. No separate Reg 30 contract disclosure is in this set. Your credibility rule says to carry no Bayview revenue until a named customer is printed (Notion, Section 4). That rule still holds.

### T4: Bayview capitalisation is speeding up, and "operational" needs a definition

What it says [FILED, Results p.8, note 5]: Bayview CWIP is ₹487.6 Cr (₹404.7 Cr at 31-Mar). That includes ₹124.6 Cr of pre-operative costs (₹58.5 Cr at 31-Mar), "as management is of the view the same is eligible for capitalisation".

- H1 pre-operative cost capitalised: ₹66.1 Cr. Notion logs Q1 at ₹29.3 Cr (Notion, V4), so Q2 was about ₹36.8 Cr, up 26% QoQ. My arithmetic, not filed.
- At the Q2 rate that is about ₹147 Cr a year. The FY28 cost wall on file assumes Bayview running costs of about ₹115-120 Cr a year move into P&L (Notion, V4 and 5.3). On the Q2 run-rate the wall is about ₹27-32 Cr higher. My arithmetic, not filed.
- The new auditor, S R Batliboi, gave a clean limited review with no emphasis of matter (Results pp.9-11). Your Ind AS 16 question on post-validation capitalisation (Notion, S11) is unanswered, not resolved. A limited review is not an audit.

The T4 leg depends on the reading. T4 fires if the "operational date moved past Mar-2027" (Tracker, T4 row).

- Reading A: "operational" means the plant is ready to run. The filing does not say that has slipped. T4 passes for now.
- Reading B: "operational" means making a customer's product. The first customer's drug substance is FY28, after March 2027. T4 fires.

Please rule. Either way, pre-operative capitalisation now runs through at least Q4 FY27. At the Q2 rate, that is about ₹74 Cr more kept out of FY27 P&L [INFERENCE on duration; my arithmetic].

### Leadership: the CFO and three Executive Committee members go in one filing

What it says [FILED, item 138, pp.1-4]:

- CFO Deepak Jain is leaving "for personal reasons" to take an outside opportunity. The press release says he stays "at least until the close of the third quarter's financial results" (press release p.4). The Reg 30 letter says the "transition timeline to be mutually agreed upon", with no last date (p.3). The two filings do not match.
- Ajay Tandon, Head of Strategy and Corporate Development and an EC member, resigns effective 30-Oct. His letter is dated 23-Oct (p.4). The disclosure is signed 27-Oct at 20:06 (p.2). On the dates in the filing, that is about three days past the 24-hour window, unless the letter reached the company later than its date [INFERENCE].
- The Heads of Chemistry (Subhendu Kumar) and Biology (Priyaranjan Pattanaik) are "redesignated" from SMP to Non-SMP from 27-Oct (p.1). The press release says both "will be moving on from Syngene" at the end of November and December (press release p.2). The Reg 30 letter does not say they are leaving.

Tested against the record:

- Churn. Notion logs a CFO change on 30-Nov-2024, two CEO changes, a CCO and CHRO from Biocon on 1-May-2026, and the Head of Corporate Affairs resigning on 21-Sep-2026 (Notion, V5). This adds a second CFO exit in about 23 months and three more EC exits. My arithmetic on the CFO tenure, from the logged date.
- The two research heads leave in the line where the thesis question sits: is research attrition recent or structural (Tracker, R2/T1 row)? The research reorganisation under the CSO took effect on 1-Oct (press release p.2).
- Promoter tripwire. "Biocon-sourced management" is a named structure concern (Notion, Section 8). Watch whether the CFO successor comes from Biocon.

### Fair value and projections

Against the projections on file (Notion, 5.1-5.2; my arithmetic, not filed):

- Revenue. H1 is ₹1,569 Cr against a base-path H1 of ₹1,526 Cr, so it is ₹43 Cr ahead. Q2's ₹833 Cr sits between base (₹790 Cr) and bull (₹840 Cr). The FY27 base of ₹3,336 Cr needs ₹1,767 Cr in H2.
- Guidance. "Single-digit decline" (about ₹3,400-3,550 Cr) needs H2 of ₹1,831-1,981 Cr, or ₹916-990 Cr a quarter. FY26 H2 was ₹1,954 Cr, and that included Zoetis. The filings do not restate FY27 guidance. "In line with expectations" (press release p.3) is not a guide. T2 is untestable until the call.
- EBITDA. The FY27 base of ₹656 Cr needs ₹418 Cr in H2, a 23.7% margin, against Q2's 17.7%.
- PAT. The FY27 base of ₹207 Cr needs ₹168 Cr in H2, against ₹38.8 Cr in H1. On the H1 run-rate, FY27 is tracking much closer to the bear case (₹20 Cr) than to the base.
- Hedge loss. The base carries a residual of about ₹135 Cr for Q2-Q4 and ₹170-200 Cr for the year (Notion, V4 and 5.2). Q2 alone was ₹61.4 Cr. If Q3 and Q4 run at a similar level, FY27 lands nearer ₹230 Cr. That fits Chain 7 (Notion, Section 7).
- Net cash. After leases of ₹440.2 Cr it is about ₹952 Cr, or about ₹23.6 a share. The ₹150-225 indicative value uses about ₹33 a share (Notion, Section 6). That leg is about ₹9-10 a share lower.
- The "mid-20s" claim. Adding back the ₹61.4 Cr hedge loss gives 25.1% [MGMT claim; my arithmetic]. That is at the edge of the CRISIL 25% trigger, not above it. T5 tests reported FY27 and FY28 margins, and reported H1 is 15.2%.

### What it does not say

- The research and CDMO split, as above.
- The Bayview customer, value and scale.
- Any FY27 guidance.
- Who received the ₹2.5 Cr H1 "investment in equity shares" (Results p.4). It is small, but it is a new line. With T6 (director-linked biotech customers) open, the counterparty matters.
- Unit 3's remaining CWIP (open item Q25, Notion Section 10). Standalone CWIP is ₹555.8 Cr (Results p.6), with no split.

### Price and verdict

₹366 (screener.in, 28-Oct-2026 06:13 IST). 52-week range ₹344-679. Market cap ₹14,782 Cr. This read is the 27-Oct close, before the market saw the results, which came after 18:30. Notion carries ₹386 (screener.in, 18-Oct-2026). Against the record (my arithmetic, not filed):

- 63% above the R3 re-engage line of about ₹225, and about 1.6x the top of the ₹150-225 indicative range.
- About 55x base FY28 EPS of ₹6.7 and 72x base FY27 EPS of ₹5.1. The FY27 base EPS is now at risk.

The single observation that separates the readings: **the research share stated on today's 28-Oct call.** At 73.2% or above, R2's first leg passes and the CDMO recovery is extra. Below 69.6%, T1 fires, and the 13% sequential gain was CDMO plus hedge timing, not a research turn.

On the business falsifiers (Notion, Section 1): no CGU impairment, and research ex-BMS is not disclosed, so none fires. T3 fires as written, pending your ruling on seasonality. R1 does not fire. T4 depends on reading. On your rules, I read this as moving the name further from re-engagement, not closer. The recognition gap is still overshot. SHALLOW WATCH posture unchanged. Decision Status is yours. That is your call.

## Raymond Realty: cash beat the tracker, margin and profit did not

Raymond Realty is in COMPANIES MASTER as WATCHLIST / AVOID, not held. Entry ₹611 ceiling, MoS ₹550, hard stop ₹520, base FV Y3 ₹1,343, Promoter CONCERN (3 Red) (Notion, properties and Role 2 recommendation). Five tracker rows are linked and all were read: three Tier 1 (Q2 margin, net debt, H1 CFO) and two Tier 2 (warrant money, Thane Jekegram launch).

Items 96, 97 and 98 are the same 15-page document filed three times (identical text: results, limited review, press release). Item 95 is the separate 46-page investor deck. All read in full, including the six image pages (all section dividers, no data).

This is the print that every open Tier 1 test was waiting for. It resolves the cash leg in the bull direction. It resolves nothing on the margin leg until you rule on the threshold, and it opens a debt-like item the net-debt lines do not count.

### What it says, in numbers [FILED]

Consolidated, ₹ Cr (Results_56eda7, p.9-11; converted from ₹ lakh):

| Line | Q2 FY27 | Q1 FY27 | Q2 FY26 | H1 FY27 | H1 FY26 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Revenue from operations | 748.5 | 526.7 | 697.2 | 1,275.2 | 1,076.5 |
| Total income | 757.5 | 535.7 | 705.8 | 1,293.2 | 1,097.7 |
| Finance costs | 43.5 | 47.2 | 25.6 | 90.7 | 40.2 |
| PBT | 66.3 | 15.2 | 70.1 | 81.5 | 91.6 |
| PAT | 53.1 | 13.4 | 60.2 | 66.5 | 76.7 |
| EPS (₹) | 7.97 | 2.02 | 9.04 | 9.99 | 11.52 |

- Balance sheet at 30-Sep: borrowings ₹767.8 Cr non-current + ₹451.9 Cr current = ₹1,219.7 Cr gross. Total equity ₹1,622.2 Cr. Inventories ₹4,998.5 Cr, up ₹717.6 Cr since March (p.10).
- H1 cash flow: net cash used in operations −₹147.0 Cr, against −₹319.1 Cr in H1 FY26. Interest paid −₹85.8 Cr sits in financing. Term loans drawn ₹297.5 Cr, repaid ₹171.9 Cr (p.11).
- Press release: Q2 EBITDA ₹115 Cr at 15%, net debt ₹912 Cr, liquidity ₹308 Cr, D/E 0.8x, cost of debt ~9.6%. Q2 interest ₹44 Cr = ₹27 Cr bank + ₹17 Cr to BMC/TMC/MHADA for FSI and approvals. Q2 booking mix JDA 57%, Thane 43% (p.13-14) [MGMT].
- Deck: H1 interest ₹91 Cr = ₹55 Cr bank + ₹36 Cr government (In_606f6a, p.22). A management cash bridge for H1: project-level operating cash +₹45 Cr, corporate and statutory −₹250 Cr, net −₹205 Cr, bank loan +₹155 Cr (p.24). Launch pipeline with dates and economic interest (p.27).
- Limited review unmodified. Subsidiary list now includes Ten X Mahalaxmi Ltd and Ten X Realty South Ltd; Chembur Realty is renamed Ten X Realty North (p.8).

### The arithmetic against the record (my arithmetic, not filed)

EBITDA margin. Q2 PBT 66.3 + finance 43.5 + depreciation 5.4 = ₹115.2 Cr. On total income that is 15.2%. Strip out other income (₹9.0 Cr) and it is 14.2% on revenue from operations. Q1 was 13.1% (11.6% ex other income). H1 is 14.3%.

Net debt. Filed borrowings ₹1,219.7 Cr less cash ₹125.8 Cr, other bank balances ₹79.2 Cr and current investments ₹98.6 Cr gives about ₹916 Cr. The company's ₹912 Cr ties within ₹4 Cr. The provisional ₹914 Cr on the tracker is confirmed. Q2 rise was +₹88 Cr against +₹168 Cr in Q1 (FY26 close ₹656 Cr on file; Q1 ₹824 Cr).

The H1 net-debt rise bridges cleanly: CFO −147, interest paid −86, capex −21, dividend −13, interest received +11 = about −₹256 Cr, against net debt ₹656 Cr to ₹912 Cr = +₹256 Cr.

D/E. Net ₹912 / equity ₹1,622 = 0.56x. Gross ₹1,220 / ₹1,622 = 0.752x. The company's "0.8x" is gross, rounded. The 0.13x on file (11-Aug addendum) is wrong; this closes the open D/E question from 7-Oct.

Thane vs JDA pre-sales, summed from the per-project pages (In_606f6a, p.30-44):
- Q2: Thane own land ₹382 Cr, JDAs ₹518 Cr (Wadala 264, BKC 123, Bandra 85, Sion 46). Ties to the 43/57 split.
- Q1: Thane ₹249 Cr, JDAs ₹448 Cr.
- H1 Thane ₹631 Cr, about ₹1,260 Cr annualised.

Collections quality. Two Thane projects with fresh OCs drove Q2 cash: Address by GS Season 1 collected ₹118 Cr (₹31 Cr in Q1), and Park Avenue, Phase 1A OC, collected ₹173 Cr (₹46 Cr in Q1) (p.32, p.37). That is ₹291 Cr, 43% of the ₹682 Cr. The 7-Oct inference that part of Q2 collections were one-time possession dues is now supported by filed figures. The cause is still my inference. JDA collections were ₹165 Cr in Q2 and ₹167 Cr in Q1. Flat.

### The gates, leg by leg

**Margin, second leg (Tier 1 row).** Q1 13.1%. Q2 15.2% on the company basis, 14.2% ex other income.
- Recommendation stop-loss, "2 consecutive Q EBITDA below 14%": does not fire on either basis.
- Monitoring #1 red, "below 15% for 2 consecutive Q": depends on reading. On the company's basis Q2 clears by 0.2 points. On revenue from operations it fails, and the stop fires.
- Devil's Advocate early warning, "Q2 FY27 EBITDA below 18%: two consecutive Q below 18% = margin case broken": fires.
- Your ruling on the threshold has been pending since 7-Oct. The print now adds a second question: the basis. Please rule on both.

One more layer. The Devil's Advocate early-warning table also logged "Q1 FY27 EBITDA margin below 15%: immediate WATCHLIST downgrade; do NOT enter" and "Any TWO firing = exit position (if entered)" (Notion, Role 3). Q1 13% lit the first. Q2 below 18% lights a second. Net debt above ₹850 Cr is a third if read as a standing level. On that table the name is an entry blocker today. On the Recommendation stop-loss list it is not. Which table governs is the real question.

**Net debt (Tier 1 row).**
- Monitoring #3, red above ₹1,000 Cr by Q2: passes. ₹912 Cr, ₹88 Cr of headroom.
- Exit ₹1,500 Cr: passes.
- Risk #2 first signal, "net debt above ₹900 Cr; D/E above 0.75x": depends on reading. As a standing level, net debt is ₹12 Cr over. Gross D/E is 0.752x, on the 0.75x line; net D/E 0.56x clears it. The 7-Oct ruling (Q1-only or standing) still decides this, and you now also need net or gross D/E.

**H1 CFO (Tier 1 row).** Tracker line: better than −₹300 Cr supports the base path (FY27 CFO −₹600 Cr). H1 is −₹147 Cr. Passes clearly. Even with interest paid added back in, −₹233 Cr still passes. The management bridge shows −₹205 Cr on its own basis (p.24). Every reading passes.

**Interest guidance (not a gate, a logged figure).** The Q1 call guided full-year interest at about ₹100-120 Cr, hedged (Notion, Q1 review). H1 finance cost is already ₹91 Cr. If the guide meant total finance cost, the run-rate is about ₹180 Cr, 50-80% above it. If it meant bank interest only, H1 bank interest of ₹55 Cr runs at about ₹110 Cr, inside it. The deck's split suggests the guide was bank-only. The P&L carries both.

**Thane Jekegram launch (Tier 2 row).** The row's bull leg needs a RERA launch on the Jekegram land inside FY27. The deck schedules "Thane Owned Land Parcel", 4.65 mn sq ft, ₹8,500 Cr, for Q2 FY28, completion FY36 (p.27) [MGMT]. On management's own schedule the FY27 bull leg fails. The ₹247 Cr of warrant money tied to Jekegram approvals (Notion, 14-Oct) is funding a launch at least two quarters past FY27.

**Mahim (monitoring #4, green = Q3 FY27 launch as guided).** Passes, but the order has flipped. The Q1 call gave Mahim-1 in Q3 and Mahim-2 in Q4 (Notion, QM-1). The deck now has Mahim-2 in Q3 and Mahim-1 in Q4 (p.27). One Mahim is still Q3, so this is not a slip under Risk #3. Mahim-1 itself has moved one quarter.

**Related-party sub-loans (monitoring #6, green ₹1,000-1,200 Cr, red above ₹1,500 Cr).** Standalone loans are ₹987.3 Cr current + ₹204.4 Cr non-current = ₹1,191.6 Cr, up from ₹1,068.3 Cr in March (Results_56eda7, p.4). That FY26 figure ties to the ₹1,068 Cr on file. Green, but ₹8 Cr from the top of the band. Standalone H1 cash flow shows ICDs given, net ₹127.6 Cr (p.5). The consolidated cash flow has no ICD line, so they are intra-group [INFERENCE].

**Stop-loss triggers on the Recommendation list.** Net debt ₹1,500 Cr: no. New Singhania governance event: none in these filings. Two quarters below 14%: no. Q1 pre-sales decline: no. Hard stop ₹520: no. None fires.

### Inflection signals (11-Aug block)

- I-1 delivery: still red. Both Q2 OCs are Thane. The JDAs are at Bandra Tower A 8th slab, BKC and Sion shore piling, Wadala excavation (p.41-44).
- I-2 collections vs bookings: still red. 76% in Q2 (682/902), 77% in H1. The Q2 ratio leans on Thane possession dues.
- I-3 net debt: rising, but the pace halved (+₹168 Cr in Q1, +₹88 Cr in Q2).
- I-5 signing pace vs delivery: the deck shows ₹185 Cr spent in H1 on "yet to be launched" projects, ₹178 Cr of it on approvals (p.24). That is the clock-reset cost. It is 15% of H1 outflow.
- I-6 PAT / OCF guidance: further along. PAT margin guide restated at 9-10% (p.10). The deck now shows a project-level H1 cash bridge (p.24). It is not a forward OCF guide, but it is new disclosure.
- I-7 CFO: negative, smaller than last year's H1.

### Fair value and projection consequences

PAT path. FY27 PAT guide 9-10% on about ₹3,600 Cr = ₹324-360 Cr (Notion, 7-Oct). H1 PAT is ₹66.5 Cr. H2 must deliver ₹258-294 Cr. H2 FY26 delivered ₹227.9 Cr (FY26 ₹304.6 Cr less H1 ₹76.7 Cr). So H2 FY27 must beat H2 FY26 by 13-29%. My arithmetic. The logged base FY27 is ₹340-370 Cr and the bear is ₹300 Cr (Notion, 11-Aug addendum). H1 does not separate base from bear. H2 does.

Margin path. FY27 EBITDA guide 17-19%. On about ₹3,600 Cr that is ₹612-684 Cr. Less H1 ₹185 Cr, H2 needs ₹427-499 Cr on about ₹2,307 Cr of income: 18.5-21.6%. H2 FY26 ran at about 18.2% (₹353 Cr on ₹1,942 Cr). My arithmetic. The guide needs H2 FY27 to beat the H2 that contained the 21.5% Q4.

Revenue. H1 revenue from operations +18.5% YoY. The "minimum 20%" guide needs H2 at about +21%.

ROCE. H1 EBIT (PBT ₹81.5 Cr + finance ₹90.7 Cr) annualised is about ₹344 Cr. On average capital employed of about ₹2,380 Cr that is about 14.5%, against the ~20% guide. My arithmetic, seasonal, H1 only.

Base FV Y3 ₹1,343 rests on FY29 PAT ₹478 Cr at 17% EBITDA (Notion, Role 1). This print does not break that. It shows the interest line is growing faster than the model's interest-drag years assumed: H1 finance cost ₹91 Cr against ₹40 Cr a year ago. The cash leg moved toward base. The P&L leg moved toward bear.

### What it does not say

- No mention of the ₹210 Cr ICICI Pru AIF deal signed 26-Oct. The instrument is still undisclosed.
- No mention of the warrants or the ₹102 Cr upfront. The ₹308 Cr liquidity excludes it. "Fully funded for the next year of construction spends" is said without either source (p.14).
- No balance of the dues owed to BMC/TMC/MHADA that carry the ₹36 Cr H1 interest.
- No reason for the Mahim order swap.
- No Q2-only cash flow. Only H1 is filed.
- Economic interest is now filed per project, but the ₹52,000 Cr GDV is not restated net of it. Parel is 55% Raymond, Wadala 62%, Kandivali 70.2%, Mahim-2 82.4%, Bandra 91% (p.26-27). The record carries Parel at the full ₹8,500 Cr.

### Internal inconsistencies

- Revenue recognised to date: ₹9,500 Cr on the GDV slide (JDA ₹1,930 Cr) against ₹10,078 Cr on the project table (JDA projects sum to ₹2,549 Cr) (p.5 vs p.26). A ₹578 Cr gap, almost all in JDAs. A plausible cause is rehab-area revenue under Ind AS 115 being left out of p.5 [INFERENCE]. It matters because p.5's "balance revenue ₹3,700 Cr" depends on it.
- BKC has recognised ₹577 Cr of revenue on ₹521 Cr of bookings and ₹64 Cr collected. Sion has recognised ₹242 Cr on ₹229 Cr of bookings (p.26). Revenue above sales is the rehab-revenue mechanic on file (Notion, Ind AS 115) [INFERENCE]. It is P&L running far ahead of cash in exactly the engine the thesis watches.
- "No new project launches during the quarter" (press release p.13), but the deck notes 13 BKC retail units launched in Q2 (p.42). Minor.
- Unsold and unlaunched GDV is "> ₹38,000 Cr" (p.4) and ₹38,800 Cr (p.9). Bharat Connect on file said over ₹39,000 Cr. Minor.
- Press release credits Q2 growth "entirely" to the "Address by GS" portfolio. Wadala is Address by GS, but BKC Invictus (₹123 Cr) and Raymond Retail (₹104 Cr) are not. Minor overstatement.

### Two readings

Bull:
- H1 CFO −₹147 Cr is less than half of last year's −₹319 Cr, and well inside the −₹300 Cr tracker line.
- The net-debt rise halved quarter on quarter. The H1 bridge ties to the cash flow statement with nothing hidden.
- Margin moved from 13% to 15%. The 14% stop-loss is clear.
- JDAs now carry 57% of bookings, and Thane is holding about ₹1,260 Cr annualised, above the Risk #5 ₹1,000 Cr floor.
- Disclosure improved: per-project economic interest, a cash bridge, and the bank vs government interest split.

Bear:
- PAT fell 12% in Q2 and 14% in H1 while pre-sales more than doubled. H1 PAT margin is 5.1% against a 9-10% guide.
- Finance cost more than doubled. The ₹17 Cr a quarter to authorities is a debt-like cost outside net debt.
- Q2 collections lean on two Thane possession events. JDA collections are flat at about ₹165 Cr a quarter and only 25% of JDA sales (₹857 Cr of ₹3,430 Cr, p.5).
- The Thane launch the warrant money funds is a Q2 FY28 event. Mahim-1 moved a quarter.
- Margin is below 18% for two quarters, and the guide needs a record H2.

The one observation that separates them: Q3 FY27 results (by mid-Feb 2027). Two numbers:
- Q3 EBITDA margin at or above 18%.
- Q3 PAT above about ₹100 Cr. That leaves Q4 needing ₹160-190 Cr to reach the 9-10% guide. This threshold is my derivation, not a logged gate.
Both met supports the base path. Q3 margin below 17% with PAT below ₹80 Cr means FY27 lands near the ₹300 Cr bear, and the base path slips a year.

### The debt-like item the lines do not count

The press release says ₹17 Cr of Q2 interest is "towards Interest to Government authorities: BMC/TMC/MHADA for Additional FSI and Approvals" (p.14). The Q1 review logged this leg at 8-9% (Notion, FND-17). At ₹17 Cr a quarter, about ₹68 Cr a year, an 8-9% rate implies about ₹750-850 Cr of deferred authority dues. My arithmetic, on a logged rate, not a filed balance. Added to ₹912 Cr, that is ₹1,660-1,760 Cr, above the ₹1,500 Cr exit line.

This is inference. The rate may differ. These dues may be installment premiums that the market and the rating agency treat as trade-like. The FY26 leg was about ₹45 Cr on file and the run-rate is now about 50% higher, so the balance is growing with the approvals push. Your net-debt lines were set on bank debt. Whether these dues belong inside them is a ruling, not a fact I can settle.

### Cross-links

- The pending before/after-equity-money rule covers Raymond, Venus, Embassy and Sambhv (Notion, 09-Oct). Raymond now has three inputs: the ₹102 Cr warrant upfront, the ₹210 Cr AIF, and the authority dues.
- Raymond Lifestyle Ltd (separate listed company, former name on this letterhead) holds its own board on 28-Oct. Check the ticker RAYMONDREL before logging.
- Sunteck Realty is on file as the cleaner alternative (own-land margins, D/E 0.21x). This print widens the margin gap the record names.

### Briefly: the deck's governance page

The deck lists independent director Virendra Singh as ex-ECL Finance (Edelweiss Group) and G Corp Developers (p.14). The record has MD Sahni's career as Edelweiss, then G Corp, then Mahindra Lifespaces (Notion, Role 3). An independent director who shares two of the MD's former employers is a question on independence quality, not a governance event. The deck also says related-party transactions get an independent third-party review and certification by E&Y. That is new to the record and a small positive for monitor #7. "Board Independence: 100%" on the slide refers to committee chairs, not the board. The record has 5 of 8 independent.

### Price

₹597 (screener.in, 28-Oct-2026 06:13 IST). 52-week range ₹349-742. Market cap ₹3,975 Cr. Notion on file ₹571 (26-Oct-2026). The board ended at 5:00 PM on 27-Oct, after the close, so this price predates the results [INFERENCE from timings].

- Up 4.6% since 26-Oct. Inside the undiluted ₹550-611 band, 2.3% below the ceiling. Above the diluted band of about ₹523-581 by ₹16 (Notion, 09-Oct). The rebase ruling again decides whether the price is in band.
- 8.5% above MoS ₹550. Above the post-Devil's-Advocate entry of ₹500-550 (Notion, Role 3).
- 14.8% above the ₹520 hard stop. Not held, so the stop is not live.
- On base FV Y3 ₹1,343, about 31.0% a year. On the diluted ₹1,277, about 28.9%. Both clear 25%. My arithmetic.
