- Trailing EPS ₹44.0 (FY26 ₹45.52 less H1 FY26 ₹11.52 plus H1 FY27 ₹9.99). P/E about 13.6x, against 14.1x on file. My arithmetic.
- Warrant price ₹614 is 2.8% above market.

### Verdict

No stop-loss trigger on the Recommendation list fires. The cash leg resolved toward the base path and better than feared. The margin leg fires on the 18% reading, depends on basis at 15%, and clears at 14%. The Devil's Advocate early-warning table now has two or three signals lit, which on its own rule blocks entry. The PAT and interest lines moved toward the bear. Promoter CONCERN (3 Red) still caps the name, so in-band is not buyable on your rules. I read this as no move toward entry, and closer to the weakened end if the 18% table governs. The 28-Oct call at 11:30 IST is the test of FND-15: whether the CFO and Group CFO answer the interest and authority-dues questions. That is your call.

## Unimech Aero.: strong quarter, cash test misses by a hair, confirmed book shrinks

Unimech is in COMPANIES MASTER at WATCHLIST / BUY ON DIPS, Position: None. Entry ₹213 and MoS ₹170 in the properties; the 04-Aug AR review still says ₹237 / ₹190, and your ruling on which is live has been pending since 04-Oct (Notion, properties and 01-Oct entry). Two tracker rows are linked (Saudi JV production date; Saudi JV second tranche). I read both. Seven items were filed on 27-Oct. Three of them (items 151, 152, 153) are the same 17-page PDF: board outcome, results, and the senior-management annexure. I read every page of all four distinct documents, plus the deck's five image pages, which are only section covers.

The results land on every operating line the file tests. Two things sit under the headline. The H1 cash test that decides the Pillar 2 upgrade comes in at 0.896x against a 0.90x bar. The ₹855 Cr "order book" is ₹266 Cr of purchase orders plus ₹589 Cr of frameworks and award letters. The purchase-order part shrank from June.

### What the results say [FILED] (Board outcome _43a4fe, consolidated p.13-15; deck _b6a12b p.6-8)

| ₹ Cr, consolidated | Q2 FY26 | Q1 FY27 | Q2 FY27 | H1 FY27 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Revenue from operations | 61.98 | 107.62 | 122.998 | 230.62 |
| EBITDA (deck, excl. other income) | 18.54 | 39.25 | 49.01 | 88.26 |
| EBITDA margin | 29.9% | 36.5% | 39.8% | 38.3% |
| Other income | 9.87 | 7.33 | 6.98 | 14.31 |
| Finance cost | 1.39 | 1.94 | 2.03 | 3.97 |
| PBT | 20.68 | 36.69 | 44.91 | 81.60 |
| PAT | 15.67 | 27.86 | 31.77 | 59.64 |
| Diluted EPS (₹) | 3.08 | 5.47 | 6.24 | 11.71 |

My arithmetic, not filed:
- Core PBT excluding other income was ₹37.93 Cr in Q2, against ₹10.81 Cr in Q2 FY26. Other income is now 15.5% of PBT. FY26 was 58.4% (Notion, Q4 FY26 review). The treasury prop the file worried about has been replaced by operations, a quarter faster than the file expected.
- Materials plus inventory change fell to 27.4% of revenue, from 32.1% in Q1. The Q1 bear counter on file was "margin lift is a mix artifact, gross margin deteriorated" (Notion, Q1 review). Q2 reverses that. The lift is now at the gross line.
- Finance cost was ₹2.03 Cr. That confirms the ₹11.25 Cr Q4 FY26 spike was one-off (Notion, Q4 review watch item 1).
- H1 effective tax rate is 25.8%, up from 20.8% in FY26. The forward ETR step-up flagged in Q1 is now in the numbers.

### The cash test: 0.896x against a 0.90x bar

The single cleanest metric on file: "If H1 FY27 CFO/PAT >0.90x on the Hobel-consolidated base, Pillar 2 moves 1.00x→1.15x, destination PE 18x→~20x" (Notion, AR review step 13).

- H1 consolidated CFO ₹53.46 Cr; H1 PAT ₹59.64 Cr (_43a4fe p.13-14). Ratio 0.896x. My arithmetic.
- Reading A, strict: the bar is "above 0.90x". It fails by 0.004x.
- Reading B, tax timing: taxes paid were ₹24.97 Cr against a P&L tax charge of ₹21.07 Cr (p.13-14). Adding back the ₹3.90 Cr advance-tax excess gives ₹57.36 Cr, or 0.96x. Passes. My arithmetic.
- Reading C, PAT before the ₹0.89 Cr associate loss: 0.883x. Fails.

Please rule on which reading the gate meant. On the bull side, FCF is positive: CFO ₹53.46 Cr less capex ₹36.52 Cr = ₹16.94 Cr (p.14, my arithmetic). Receivables rose ₹20.28 Cr inside working capital. Monitoring threshold 7 (CFO/PAT above 0.80) is green on any reading. The 1.15x upgrade is your call either way. If you apply it, the live-entry ruling (₹213 vs ₹237) becomes binding, because the page says the upgrade moves entry in opposite directions in two places (Notion, 01-Oct inconsistency note).

### Thesis-broken triggers, leg by leg

- (a) FY27 consolidated revenue below ₹400 Cr: passes. H1 is ₹230.62 Cr, 58% of ₹400 Cr. H2 needs ₹169.4 Cr, about ₹84.7 Cr a quarter, against a ₹123 Cr Q2. The green line (above ₹420 Cr) needs ₹189.4 Cr. My arithmetic. My 23-Oct estimate on file was Q2 near ₹119 Cr and H1 near ₹227 Cr (Notion, 23-Oct entry). Actual is slightly above both.
- (b) Blended EBITDA below 22% for two quarters: passes. 39.8%, above the 34-35% FY27 guide and above the bull projection's 35.3% (Notion, Q1 concall; Role 1 projections).
- (c) Hobel EBITDA below 40%: untestable, for the third quarter. The results still report one segment (_43a4fe p.16, note 3). The deck gives no Hobel revenue or margin. The 28-Oct call is the third chance to ask.
- (d) CRISIL persistence plus a new governance event: persistence leg confirmed again. The deck's "Credit Rating Validation" box cites CARE A and a D&B 5A rating, and does not mention CRISIL (_b6a12b p.6). New-event leg: depends on reading, see the disclosure section below.

Monitoring thresholds (Notion, Role 2): revenue green-leaning; blended EBITDA green; Hobel unknown; utilisation about 65% [MGMT, p.20] against a ">70% by mid-FY27" green line, so amber (not red: red is below 60%); WC green (receivables about 91 days on Q2 annualised revenue, my arithmetic); CRISIL red; CFO/PAT green.

### The order book: "up 8 times" is a basis switch

The press release headline says "Consolidated Order Book at ₹8,546 Mn up 8-times YoY" (_702d9f p.2). The deck shows the build (_b6a12b p.7):
- Confirmed POs ₹266 Cr at Sep-26, against ₹105 Cr at Sep-25: up 153%.
- Plus "LTA & Award Letter ~Rs. 589 Cr", giving ~₹855 Cr.
- ₹855 Cr / ₹105 Cr = 8.1x. That compares a total that includes frameworks with a prior-year figure of confirmed POs only. Like for like, the growth is 153%. My arithmetic.

Against the file: the Q1 book was ₹280.3 Cr (Notion, Q1 review; CARE's 17-Sep release also cites ₹280 Cr at 30-Jun). So confirmed POs fell about 5% quarter on quarter. Implied Q2 intake ≈ ₹266 Cr − ₹280.3 Cr + ₹123.0 Cr revenue = about ₹109 Cr, a book-to-bill of about 0.88x. My arithmetic, not filed. It assumes June used the same confirmed-PO basis. Please confirm that on the call.

What the ₹589 Cr contains [FILED, p.7]: FACC USD 7.5M; Honeywell 10-year LTA, no value; a new "~USD 43 million" award letter from an unnamed "Aerospace Tier-1 manufacturer" (p.6 calls it "French Tier-1"). At ₹96.5 per USD, the rate implied by the filed Saudi ODI (₹39.36 Cr for SAR 15.30M, SAR pegged at 3.75), FACC plus the award is about ₹487 Cr. The residual is about ₹102 Cr. That is the closest the company has come to sizing Honeywell. [INFERENCE] It is probably the lifetime value of a "may issue purchase orders" framework (Notion, 26-Oct entry), so about ₹10 Cr a year over ten years. Against a ₹492 Cr Q2 revenue run-rate, that is small. The 26-Oct separating observation ("a disclosed number by the Q3 FY27 call") is half met: implied, not stated.

The deck's pie charts do not reconcile with either base. If the mix is on ₹855 Cr, aerospace is about ₹552 Cr, less than the ₹589 Cr of aerospace LTAs alone. If it is on ₹266 Cr, Hobel is about ₹27 Cr against ₹100.1 Cr in June (Notion, Q1 review). The basis is not stated.

Two readings:
- Bull [FILED + MGMT]: 250 FAIs done and 346 in the pipeline (p.7). Three named frameworks in four months. The MD's letter says the mix is moving "from short-cycle tooling into recurring, high-volume 'flying part' manufacturing" (p.5). PCA is 91% of the book (p.7). The file's RRM line "PO-to-PO deferrable" (Notion, Role 1) is the thing this would fix.
- Bear [INFERENCE]: the only hard backlog, confirmed POs, shrank while revenue jumped. 69% of the headline is frameworks and award letters, at least one of which has no committed volume. Revenue at ₹123 Cr a quarter is running ahead of a ₹266 Cr confirmed book, about 2.2 quarters of cover.
- The ONE observation that separates them: the Dec-26 confirmed-PO book in the Q3 FY27 deck (about Feb-2027). Below ₹266 Cr while frameworks stay in the headline confirms the bear reading. A rise above about ₹300 Cr, with first FACC or award-letter POs moving into the confirmed column, confirms the bull reading.

### What it does to fair value

- Q2 diluted EPS ₹6.24 annualises to about ₹25. The FY29 base EPS on file is ₹26.0, and the bull is ₹36.3 (Notion, Role 1). The company is already running at the FY29 base before the QIP dilution the file expects (about 13-15%, Notion FY30 scenario). Q2 EBITDA annualises to about ₹196 Cr against the FY29 base ₹220 Cr. The margin (39.8%) is above the bull case (35.3%). My arithmetic. The FY29 projection table is stale on the low side. That is a re-underwrite item, not a price signal.
- It does not change the conclusion. At ₹1,692, the price is about 68x annualised Q2 EPS. Even the bull EPS of ₹36.3 at the 20x optimistic multiple gives ₹726 (Notion, target-price matrix). That is 57% below the price. To justify ₹1,692 at 18x needs EPS of about ₹94. My arithmetic.
- Balance sheet: net cash has gone. Treasury (current investments, cash, other bank balances) fell from ₹591.8 Cr to ₹226.4 Cr. Borrowings rose from ₹112.09 Cr to ₹160.98 Cr. Net cash excluding leases is about ₹65 Cr, from about ₹480 Cr (p.12, my arithmetic). The Saudi cheque of ₹39.36 Cr left on 01-Oct, after the close (p.16, note 8). The RRM line "net cash consumed by Hobel/Saudi → neutral" (Notion, Role 1) is now fact.
- Hobel price allocation is now visible: goodwill ₹321.99 Cr, intangibles up to ₹49.22 Cr from ₹1.87 Cr (p.12). Cash out was "Purchase of Partnership interest" ₹424.80 Cr (p.14). [INFERENCE] That squares the "₹450 Cr all-cash" in the deck (p.12) with the AR's "equity + debt + CCDs" (Notion, AR Note 51): external cash about ₹425 Cr; the CCDs and loans are intra-group routing. The parent's own cash flow shows ₹148.00 Cr into CCDs of a subsidiary (standalone p.6). That matches CARE's ₹148 Cr figure the Q1 review could not reconcile. The ₹25 Cr gap to ₹450 Cr may be the 10% holdback. That is inference; the holdback is not separately shown.

### What the filings do not say

- Hobel's Q2 revenue and margin. Organic growth is therefore not filed. If Hobel ran at about ₹33 Cr a quarter (Notion, 23-Oct arithmetic from the Q1 call's ₹22 Cr for two months), organic Q2 was about ₹90 Cr, up about 45% on ₹62 Cr. [INFERENCE]
- The intra-group Hobel transfer price. The board approved the parent buying 60,80,000 Hobel shares from Innomech, so that Hobel becomes a direct WOS (_43a4fe p.1). It says Reg 30 details "will be submitted post completion". Price, mode and valuation are not given. [INFERENCE] If the parent pays cash, Innomech, which carries almost all group debt (Notion, AR Note 20), can repay borrowings with it.
- The new USD 43M award letter. About ₹415 Cr at the implied rate, 1.7x FY26 revenue (my arithmetic). Customer unnamed, date not given. I find no separate Reg 30 filing for it in any run INDEX on disk (runs start 09-Oct) or in the Notion record. Its first appearance on file is an investor deck.
- The parent now borrows. Standalone term loans of ₹13.27 Cr were drawn and ₹12.02 Cr is outstanding (standalone p.4, p.7). The AR logged "standalone parent has ZERO borrowings" (Notion, AR step 5). Standalone CFO was negative ₹22.99 Cr (p.6). Standalone receivables went from ₹5.15 Cr to ₹19.93 Cr on H1 revenue of ₹21.54 Cr. The parent's purpose and the counterparty for those receivables are not given.
- Saudi production date. The deck says "advancing plans to establish" the facility (p.6), but does not repeat the late-Mar / early-Apr 2027 target.
- CRISIL. Silent again.

### Disclosure and internal inconsistencies

- Saudi JV size. The deck says "Joint Investment in JV: ~USD 30 Mn" (p.14). The filed total is SAR 60M, about USD 16M (Notion, 01-Oct entry). That is almost double. The same deck calls the first tranche "~USD 4mn" (p.6). That is Unimech's share alone. It supports Reading B in your pending "USD 10M" ruling: management itself counts Unimech's own cheque, which puts delivery 59% short (Notion, 01-Oct entry).
- Uniflux. The auditor lists it as a "Subsidiary", not a wholly owned one (p.10). The balance sheet shows a ₹1.11 lakh non-controlling interest and ₹1.00 lakh of subsidiary shares issued (p.12, p.15). The AR review logged it as "100% WOS" (Notion, AR step 5). The minority holder is not named.
- CARE monitoring report (_05d8a3). The cover says "Deviation from the objects: None" (p.3). The company's own Reg 32 statement filed the same evening says "Yes" (_175220 p.2). That is the same cover-versus-body split the Q1 review flagged. The CARE progress table's total row shows ₹153.30 Cr utilised at the start of the quarter (p.10). The line items sum to ₹247.80 Cr. My arithmetic. ₹153.30 Cr + ₹2.20 Cr = ₹155.50 Cr, which is the Mar-26 figure (₹250 Cr less the ₹94.50 Cr undeployed in AR Note 48). So the total row looks stale. Shareholder approval is dated 17-Dec-2025 by the company and 19-Dec-2025 by CARE.
- Hobel consolidation is "wef 1st May 2026" in the deck (p.6, p.8) and "from April 27, 2026" in the auditor's report (p.10). Exports are "~95%" on p.18 and 90% on p.7. Both minor.
- One good-hygiene item. Prathap B. Naidu (AGM to GM, overall charge of the Toolings Division, moved to Innomech, ceases to be SMP from 1-Nov) was disclosed the day the board approved it, five days before it takes effect (_43a4fe p.1, p.17). Contrast this with the 07-Sep SMP exits disclosed 8-14 months late (Notion, Key Notes). One oddity: the deck names promoter-WTD Preetham S V as "Head of Tooling Division" (p.21), while Naidu gets "overall responsibility for the Toolings Division". The reporting line is not stated.

Trigger (d), new-event leg, depends on reading:
- Reading A: none of these is a breach. Award letters are not always Reg 30 events. The Hobel move is intra-group. The deck numbers are marketing.
- Reading B: a USD 43M award equal to 1.7x last year's revenue, first surfacing in a deck with no Reg 30 filing, is the most material disclosure item on file since the CRISIL migration. It joins the 07-Sep late SMP exits and the 17-Oct incomplete pledge form, and you have not yet ruled on whether those count cumulatively.
- Please rule. The separating observation: whether the company files a Reg 30 for the award, or states its date and its Reg 30 treatment on the 28-Oct call. No filing by the Q3 FY27 results (about Feb-2027) would confirm Reading B.

### IPO money is fully spent

CARE says all ₹250 Cr is utilised. Only ₹2.20 Cr moved in Q2: ₹0.58 Cr to GCP, ₹1.62 Cr to issue expenses (_05d8a3 p.9-10). The M&A object (₹61.29 Cr) was fully used on 27-Apr-2026, 27 days late (p.11). Consequences:
- [INFERENCE] The 01-Oct Saudi cheque cannot have come from IPO proceeds. It came from treasury. The tracker row's falsifying test ("second tranche funded from IPO proceeds without disclosure") can no longer fire. The row needs a new test.
- The monitoring-agency regime, which caught the commingling and the ICDR 7(3) breach, should end with this report [INFERENCE]. Future capital deployment, including the ₹750 Cr QIP when it comes, loses that independent check unless a new issue appoints one.

### Dheya

Dheya raised money from outside investors in Q2. Unimech's stake fell to 27.24% (p.16, note 6), from 35.07% after the ₹3 Cr secondary purchase (Notion, 08-Sep entry). Unimech did not take part in the primary round. Outside investors took about 22% of the post-money company. My arithmetic. The Q1 call promised "Dheya USD 10M raise with UNIMECH participation" (Notion, Q1 concall). The raise happened. Participation was secondary only, and the round size is not disclosed. Unimech's share of Dheya's loss was ₹0.75 Cr in Q2 alone, almost double the ₹0.41 Cr for all of FY26 (Notion, AR step 5).

### Promise ledger (Notion, Q1 concall baseline)

- Q2 stronger: delivered.
- FY27 EBITDA 34-35%: H1 38.3%, ahead.
- Other income "to about half again" (about ₹3.7 Cr): missed in the good direction, ₹6.98 Cr.
- WC 160+ days: better than promised, about 91 days of receivables.
- Utilisation about 58%: now about 65% [MGMT].
- ROCE about 14.3% annualised: H1 14.9% (p.24).
- Saudi USD 10M in Aug: late and short, as already logged.
- Hobel AS9100 by Q4 FY27: Hobel certifications listed are ISO 9001, IATF 16949 and ISO 14001 only (p.13). Open.
- Engine Tier-1 LTA: Honeywell, value only implied.
- Dheya raise with participation: secondary only.

### Price

₹1,692 (screener.in, 28-Oct-2026 06:14 IST). 52-week ₹695 to ₹1,901. Market cap ₹8,610 Cr. This is the 27-Oct close. The results came out after the market closed (board ended 20:00, signed 21:07 IST, _43a4fe p.2), so the price has not reacted yet. Up 4.7% from ₹1,616 on file (26-Oct). It is 7.9x the ₹213 entry and 7.1x the ₹237 figure. Market cap is about 2.4x the file's FY30 base value of about ₹3,600 Cr (Notion, FY30 scenario). My arithmetic. Price does not invite action.

### Verdict

No thesis-broken trigger fires. On operations this is the best quarter on file. Organic growth, margin, other-income replacement and finance cost all broke the bull way. The FY29 base projection now looks low. Against that, the cash upgrade misses by 0.004x on a strict reading, net cash is gone, the confirmed book fell, and the disclosure layer added three new questions: the unfiled USD 43M award, the "USD 30 Mn" JV, and the undisclosed Hobel transfer price. On your rules the name stays WATCHLIST / BUY ON DIPS, and the price gap is too wide for any of this to matter for action. That is your call. The 28-Oct 11:00 call is where the award date, the June order-book basis, the Hobel margin and CRISIL get tested.

## Permanent Magnet: filed transcript confirms QMPL stake halving; price now pays for Q2

Permanent Magnets is in COMPANIES MASTER as AVOID, posture DEEP WATCH, no position (Notion, properties: Position Size blank, Avg Cost blank). Entry Price Max ₹361, MoS ₹252, CMP on file ₹857 (screener, 25-Oct). The 25-Oct Role 4 review read the call from an auto-generated transcript "with errors" (Notion, Role 4 Sources). This item is the company's own transcript of the same 24-Oct call, filed 27-Oct (Permanent_Magnet_..._755c25.pdf, p.1). So the test is simple: what does the filed record confirm, correct or add to what is logged?

What it says, in numbers [MGMT unless marked]:
- Automotive revenue ₹24 Cr in Q2, ₹15 Cr in Q1, ₹10.5 Cr in Q2 FY26 (CFO and MD, p.17 of transcript, PDF p.18). Matches the tracker row.
- "We have orders for H2. We do not have orders for H1 of FY28" (p.18). FY28 EV view is "a judgment... not a concrete forecast" (p.17).
- FY27 revenue growth "somewhere between 20% to 30%" (p.5-6). FY27 EBITDA margin "between 20% to 25%" for the full year (p.7-8).
- Alloys: FY27 ₹40-50 Cr, H1 about ₹12 Cr, FY28 "maybe ₹70-80 Cr" (p.6, p.8, p.9). Delay costs are being paid to customers; amount not given (p.8). Gross margin 10-15% on virgin alloys, 15-25% on returns (p.6).
- Relays: "approved as per the [IEC] standard"; customers want more than the standard, so redesign; no buyer-seller agreement until after pilot lots (p.13-15). Relay gross margin "somewhere between 15% to 20%" (p.12). FY28 ₹40-50 Cr "possible" (p.12).
- Quantum (QMPL): "We do not get any revenue this year, but that it will get pushed to next year" (p.7). FY28 ₹10-40 Cr (p.12). Phase 2 capacity ₹40-60 Cr of revenue (p.12). The ₹400 Cr ECMS capex is machinery only and "a 100% investment to be done from Quantum Magnetics" (p.10-11).
- QMPL stake: asked "we will get 49% or 51% of that?", the MD says "Permanent Magnets' shareholding there is going to be 50%, 49%-50%" (p.11).
- Phase 3 funding: "we are requesting the customer to sign agreements with us and to fund it", repaid "by way of amortization over the product" (p.14).
- FY28 numbers: "I will be able to tell you maybe better after three months" (p.4). That points to the Q3 call.
- Plant consolidation 2 more years away; building about ₹30 Cr; land about ₹25 Cr already spent; rent saving about ₹5 Cr a year (p.16).
- Exports 49%, domestic 51% of Q2 revenue (p.7).

### What the filed record changes

The QMPL stake is now on a company-filed document. The tracker row logged the 49-50% line as "[MGMT, auto-transcript, garbled]" (tracker, QMPL ownership row). The company edited and filed this transcript, and the sentence stands. It is still a management statement, not a filed agreement. There is still no Reg 30 filing of a partner, price or board approval, and the Q2 auditor report still calls QMPL a wholly owned subsidiary (Notion, Role 4 Step 0D). But the "garbled" defence is gone.

The context also matters. The 49-50% answer follows a question about the ₹400 Cr QMPL programme, and the MD separately says Phase 3 waits until Phase 2 succeeds commercially (p.10-11). That gives two readings of trigger (c), "NdFeB raise diluting >30% at a pre-success price" (Notion, FINALIZE v2):
- Literal reading: (c) is about a PML share issue. No PML issue is proposed. Does not fire.
- Look-through reading: PML's share of the NdFeB slice falls from 100% to about 50%, a dilution above 30%. Whether it is "pre-success" depends on timing. If the partner enters before Phase 2 earns revenue, it fires. If it enters at Phase 3, after first Phase 2 invoices, it arguably does not.
- Please rule. The ruling has been open since 25-Oct, and the evidence for the stake cut is now firmer.

Fair value effect, using the page's own sensitivity: a halved stake takes about ₹141 a share off the full-plan slice (Notion, Role 4 Step 7). My arithmetic, not filed: TTM RRM ₹744 becomes about ₹603; H1-annualised ₹900 becomes about ₹759; Q2-annualised ₹1,062 becomes about ₹921.

Quantum FY27 revenue: the tracker falsifier "zero NdFeB revenue by FY27 close" (Notion, Step 5.5A, Quantum row) is not formally due until March. But the MD now says plainly there is no revenue this year. That is a stronger statement than the "may or may not happen" logged on 25-Oct.

### The guide does not add up to its own parts

My arithmetic, not filed. FY26 revenue was ₹225.46 Cr (Notion, Role 4 Step 1). A 20-30% year means ₹270.6-293.1 Cr, so H2 needs ₹134.9-157.4 Cr after H1's ₹135.69 Cr. The MD's own alloys number puts H2 alloys at ₹28-38 Cr (₹40-50 Cr less about ₹12 Cr in H1).
- At the top of the guide and the bottom of alloys, H2 non-alloy revenue is about ₹129 Cr.
- At the 25% mid-point, it is about ₹113 Cr.
- If the non-alloy business only held Q2's level for two quarters, it would be about ₹120-133 Cr. H1 alloys were about ₹12 Cr in total, so Q2 non-alloy revenue was at least ₹60 Cr (all alloys in Q2) and about ₹66 Cr if alloys split evenly.

So the guide either expects alloys to miss again, or expects meters and auto to soften in H2. Management did not say which. The margin guide points the same way. The MD says the full year could be "a little bit lower" than H1 (p.7). Alloys at 10-25% gross margin, against a company gross margin of 52.7% in Q2 (Notion, Role 4 Step 1), dilute the mix as they grow. The new CFO said "For the first half, EBITDA margin was 25%" (p.7). The filed H1 operating margin was 21.9% (23.3% with other income). 25% is the Q2 figure rounded. That is a minor slip on his first call, not a flag.

### Two readings

Bull [MGMT + FILED]: auto orders are in hand for H2. One customer is putting the same platform into more products (p.17). The MD says he is "quite bullish" on EV, citing fuel prices and barriers to Chinese EVs (p.16). Alloys and relays both have FY28 numbers attached (₹70-80 Cr, ₹40-50 Cr). Phase 3 may be funded by customers rather than PML equity.

Bear [MGMT + INFERENCE]: no FY28 auto orders. The guide implies H2 non-alloy revenue below Q2. Both new engines carry low gross margins: relays 15-20%, alloys 10-25%. The ₹75 Cr relay option at 45% (Notion, FINALIZE v2) has no margin assumption on the page that I could find (NOT FOUND). At 15-20% gross margin, each rupee of relay revenue is worth much less than a rupee of export auto revenue. QMPL is moving to about 50%, and the transcript does not say whether the partner is the customer, a financial investor, or who sets the price.

The observation that separates them is unchanged: Q3 FY27 auto revenue of ₹22 Cr or more with standalone operating margin of 20% or more, against ₹15 Cr or less (tracker, auto revenue row; Q3 results about late Jan to mid-Feb 2027). Add one item to the same print: the FY28 revenue number the MD promised "after three months". If no number comes with the Q3 call, treat it as a soft miss on a dated promise.

### Price

₹1,092 (screener.in, 28-Oct-2026 06:12 IST). 52-week range ₹619-1,145, market cap ₹939 Cr. My arithmetic, not filed:
- Up 27.4% from ₹857 on file (25-Oct). This is the first price after the Saturday results.
- 3.0x the ₹361 Entry Price Max. MoS ₹252 is far away.
- 46.8% above the TTM RRM value of ₹744, 21.3% above the H1-annualised ₹900, and 2.8% above the Q2-annualised ₹1,062 (Notion, Role 4 Step 7).
- 40.9x TTM operating EPS of ₹26.7; 23.5x Q2-annualised ₹46.4.
- With the QMPL stake halved, the Q2-annualised RRM value is about ₹921, and the price is 18.6% above it.

The 25-Oct review warned: "If the market capitalises Q2 annualised, it is pricing a forecast the customer has not given" (Notion, Role 4 Unasked). That is now where the price sits. The Q2-annualised base also assumes a 100% QMPL stake, which the MD has now contradicted on the filed record.

### Verdict

No thesis-broken trigger fires on the transcript. Trigger (c) needs your ruling and the evidence for it got firmer. The relay falsifier fired on 25-Oct, and the transcript confirms it: no agreement and no FY28 number. AVOID holds on the hurdle on every base, and the price has moved further away from every fair value on file. The pending FV re-run (TTM vs run-rate) matters less now: even the most generous base sits below the price once the QMPL statement is applied. That is your call.

## Sical Logistics: second Tata Capital lease line lands before H1 print

Sical is in COMPANIES MASTER at WATCHLIST / AVOID. Entry ₹12 to ₹15, MoS ₹11.9, Position "None (AVOID)", Promoter Verdict CONCERN (Notion, properties). One tracker row is linked (Axis pledge share cover), and I read it. Three items, but two are one document: items 122 and 123 are word-for-word the same board-outcome letter, same signature time 16:16:20 (07b546.txt p.1; 84b0d4.txt p.1). Item 121 is the execution disclosure with the Annexure A table. All pages read.

### What the filings say [FILED]

- Board met 27-Oct-2026, 3:30 pm to 4:00 pm. Approved a lease facility up to ₹81 Cr and a working capital demand loan (WCDL) up to ₹10 Cr, both from Tata Capital Ltd (07b546.txt p.1).
- Both agreements were executed the same day. The execution letter is signed 21:05, five hours after the board letter (dd0cd4.txt p.1).
- Lease: 60 months. Proceeds "utilized for the taking of Goods on lease as described/to be described in the relevant Purchase Order". At the end, Sical must buy the equipment at 5.00% of original cost plus taxes (dd0cd4.txt p.2).
- WCDL: ₹10 Cr, 12 months, for working capital. Security is a first pari passu charge by hypothecation over the company's current assets (dd0cd4.txt p.2 to p.3).
- Not a related party, no shareholding, no board nominee (dd0cd4.txt p.2 to p.3).

### The question the filing does not answer: is this new, or the 30-Jul facility?

On 30-Jul the board approved a ₹72 Cr equipment lease from Tata Capital, 60 months, mandatory purchase at end. The Notion entry closes with "Master lease agreement details deferred to execution date" (Notion, Run 2026-07-30). No execution of that facility is logged on the page. Today's filing calls itself "Master Lease Agreement", same lender, same tenor, same buy-out structure. It refers only to "our intimation dated October 27, 2026" (dd0cd4.txt p.1). It does not mention 30-Jul.

Two readings, and they give very different numbers (my arithmetic, not filed):

- Reading A, additive. Tata Capital lines total ₹72 Cr + ₹81 Cr + ₹10 Cr = ₹163 Cr. That is 1.46x the ₹111.30 Cr of land held for sale whose proceeds were meant to repay borrowings (Notion, Land section). The deleveraging story is then not just "two-thirds recycled" (Notion, 30-Jul). It is more than fully recycled before the land is sold.
- Reading B, upsized replacement. The 30-Jul ₹72 Cr approval was never executed and is now superseded at ₹81 Cr plus a ₹10 Cr WCDL. Total ₹91 Cr, 82% of the held-for-sale land.

Please rule, or let me verify: the cheapest test is whether an execution filing for the 30-Jul ₹72 Cr facility exists on the exchange. If none exists, reading B is more likely. If one exists, reading A holds.

### Sizing against the logged figures (my arithmetic, not filed)

- ₹81 Cr is 13.2% of the ₹615 Cr market cap (screener.in, 28-Oct-2026 06:13 IST) and 21% of FY26 revenue ₹386 Cr (Notion, Run 2026-07-28). With the WCDL, ₹91 Cr is 14.8% of market cap.
- The 30-Jul entry put annual cash outgo on ₹72 Cr at ₹18 to 20 Cr, at a 12 to 14% implied rate (Notion, 30-Jul sizing, itself an assumption). Scaled to ₹81 Cr that is about ₹20 to 23 Cr a year. The filing gives no rate, so this only carries the logged assumption forward.
- On reading A, both leases cost about ₹38 to 43 Cr a year. That is 10 to 11% of FY26 revenue, and 7 to 8% of the FY27 base revenue of ₹520 Cr (Notion, Run 2026-09-02).

### Which contract is this fleet for?

The filing does not name the equipment or the contract. Two candidates on file [INFERENCE]:

- CCL Dhori: 5 years, about ₹453 Cr before GST, about ₹91 Cr a year, LoA dated 1-Sep (Notion, 02-Sep). A 60-month lease matches a 5-year contract. ₹81 Cr is about 18% of contract value.
- SAIL Dalli: ₹39.23 Cr ex GST, 5-Oct-2026 to 18-Nov-2028 (Notion, 05-Oct). A ₹81 Cr fleet would be about twice the whole contract value, and a 60-month lease is longer than a 25-month job. Dalli alone does not explain this size.

This matters for the separating test set on 5-Oct. That review wrote: "A new equipment-finance filing for Dalli before then favours the bear" (Notion, Filing Review 05-Oct). A new equipment-finance filing has now come before the H1 print. It is not named as Dalli, and the size points to CCL. So the bear marker has fired in form. Whether it fires in substance depends on which contract the fleet serves, which the filing does not say.

### Gate test: the four AVOID-reversal conditions (Notion, Thesis-broken tripwires)

AVOID reverses only if all four hold.

- Price at ₹12 to ₹15: fails. ₹77.1 (screener.in, 28-Oct-2026 06:13 IST), 5.1x the ₹15 top of entry.
- Standalone parent self-funds for two quarters with no asset sale: not tested, and harder. The 30-Jul entry said a lease "raises the bar" on this leg (Notion, 30-Jul decision). A second lease line raises it again. Mining sits in the parent, so the lease cost lands in the standalone P&L that this leg tests [INFERENCE, consistent with Notion 05-Oct].
- Promoter pledge below 40%: passes on the 05-Oct filing and the Sep-2026 shareholding (20.32% of promoter holding) (tracker row, last observation 20-Oct). This filing does not touch it, but the price does. See below.
- FLAG-CASH resolution: not resolved, and reinforced. A 12-month demand loan for working capital, secured on current assets pari passu with other lenders, says working capital is tight while PSU receivables build [INFERENCE]. The filing gives no live credit rating, which is half of the FLAG-CASH test.

One of four legs passes. AVOID stays over-determined.

### Cross-link: the Axis pledge cover at ₹77

The tracker row says cover falls below the 1.25x line at about ₹89.3 and that "No top-up with price below Rs 89 means the facility is undrawn or cover not binding (bull)" (tracker, Falsifying Observation). My arithmetic, not filed, on the ₹85 Cr facility and 1.25x need, both [SECONDARY] on file:

- 1,19,00,000 pledged shares × ₹77.1 = ₹91.7 Cr, or 1.08x on ₹85 Cr if fully drawn. Price is 13.7% below the ₹89.3 line.
- To restore 1.25x, about 18.8 lakh more shares would be needed (2.4% of capital). Pledge would then be about 23.5% of promoter holding. That is still well under 40%.
- The 40% leg re-fires only if a fully drawn facility is cover-tested near ₹45 a share. So a top-up at today's price would hurt sentiment but would not re-fire the tripwire.

Today's new borrowing raises the stakes on whether the Axis facility is drawn. The filing does not say.

### Two readings

Bull:
- Tata Capital has now underwritten Sical twice in three months. That is a repeat credit check by a large NBFC on a post-insolvency operator (Notion, 30-Jul called the first one "a mild positive credit signal").
- Fleet on lease means the CCL and SECL contracts are being equipped, not left idle. Order book about ₹5,174 Cr (Notion, 05-Oct).
- A buy-out at 5% of cost means Sical ends up owning the fleet.

Bear:
- The lessor calls Sical "the borrower" and itself "the lender" in a lease table (dd0cd4.txt p.2). The filing's own words confirm the lease is debt in substance, which is the 30-Jul reading.
- New debt-equivalent lands before any of the ₹111 Cr held-for-sale land is reported sold.
- Price is down 15.5% from ₹91.19 on file and 8.6% from ₹84.40 on 19-Oct (tracker), into the zone where the Axis cover is tested.

The one observation that separates them: the H1 FY27 standalone results and cash-flow statement (due by mid-Nov 2026, tracker next check 14-Nov). Standalone pre-exceptional profit with lease liabilities under ₹100 Cr favours the bull (reading B, one facility). Lease liabilities near ₹150 Cr or more, or a top-up pledge filing by PLIL, confirms the bear.

### Fair value consequence

The FY28 base owners EPS of about ₹2.75 rests on depreciation ₹62 Cr "full-year lease" and interest ₹25 Cr with "some paydown from FY27 land proceeds" (Notion, 02-Sep FY28 waterfall). That build has one lease in it, the ₹72 Cr.

- On reading A, a second lease of ₹20 to 23 Cr a year is not in the base. Unless its fleet earns revenue not already in the ₹700 Cr FY28 base, it comes out of the ₹53 Cr base PBT. Then owners EPS falls below ₹2.75 and FV falls below ₹24 to ₹34 [INFERENCE, direction only; I have not re-run the waterfall].
- On reading B, the base is roughly intact, with a ₹9 Cr larger lease and a new ₹10 Cr WCDL.

Either way, it does not help. At ₹77.1 the stock is about 2.3x the ₹34 additive FV on file.

### What it does not say

- Interest rate or implied lease IRR.
- Which equipment, which contract, and how much is drawn.
- Security on the lease. Row 9 is "Not Applicable" for the lease column (dd0cd4.txt p.2).
- Whether it replaces or adds to the 30-Jul ₹72 Cr approval.
- Which other lenders share the pari passu charge on current assets.

### Inside the filings

- Same-day execution, five hours after a 30-minute board meeting, means terms were agreed before the board met [INFERENCE]. That is normal for a sanctioned facility. It is not a flag.
- The board letter said details "will be disclosed in due course upon execution" (07b546.txt p.1). Execution came the same evening. On time.

### Price context

₹77.1 (screener.in, 28-Oct-2026 06:13 IST). 52-week ₹60.1 to ₹124, market cap ₹615 Cr. On file ₹91.19 (Trendlyne, 01-Oct-2026). My arithmetic: down 15.5% from the page, 5.1x the top of entry, 28% above the 52-week low.

### Verdict

No thesis-broken trigger fires. No AVOID-reversal leg moves toward passing. The standalone self-funding leg gets harder, and FLAG-CASH gets heavier. On your rules the name stays WATCHLIST / AVOID, nearer the weak end. The open ruling that matters is whether this is a second lease or the first one executed. That is your call.

## Pitti Engg.: castings guide held by changing what it measures

Pitti is in COMPANIES MASTER as a WATCHLIST placeholder, "not a call" (Notion, Status). There is no Entry, MoS or Avg Cost, because no Role 1 valuation has run. Promoter Verdict is CAUTION / TRUSTWORTHY-with-flags. I read all three linked Tier 1 tracker rows.

What the filing is [FILED, item 87, p.1]: the transcript of the 23-Oct Q2 FY27 call, 21 pages, signed 27-Oct at 13:54. That is inside the five-working-day window, three days earlier than the about-30-Oct date on file (Notion, 24-Oct review). The Screener summary calls it a capex approval. It is not; the capex was approved on 22-Oct and is already on file. The 23-Oct review left three questions for this call. Here is what it answered, all [MGMT].

### Castings row: the guide was kept, the yardstick was moved

The row fires if Q2 castings and machined components "comes in well under ~4,600 MT" (Tracker, castings row). Q2 was 3,086 MT, which fired the row on 23-Oct (Notion, Q2 review).

What management said:

- Asked if the 17,000 MT is the sum of the castings line and the "other machined components into laminations" line, the MD said "Yes" (p.6). On that reading Q2 is 4,145 MT. Still under 4,600 MT.
- Later he said the opposite: "No." 17,000 MT is "the casting produced to make those sales", before machining (pp.19-20). Machining removes "30% to 40% of the weight" (p.17). On that basis Q2 is "closer to 4,900 tons of casting produced" (p.17).
- Asked for H1 against the 17,000 MT: "I need to pull that out... it's not there on this table" (p.21). Production numbers are promised "for the next call".
- On guidance: "FY27, I think we'll still hold on to the upgraded guidance" (p.13). Q3 "is always a little bit of challenge"; Q4 should be strong.
- The split does not add up. Machined components "about 1,600 tons, 1,700 tons", raw castings "about 1,000 tons" (p.13). That is 2,600-2,700 MT against 3,086 MT filed. Later it is "1,000 raw, remaining 2,000 machined" (p.20).

Leg test:

- As written (sales line, 3,086 MT; or 4,145 MT with the other line): fails.
- On management's new production basis (about 4,900 MT for Q2): passes for the quarter. But it cannot be checked against any filed table. H1 on that basis is not given.

Please rule on which basis the row tests. My read: a guide that is defined after the miss, on a metric not in the filing, is a promise-vs-delivery item, not a pass. The row's own wording reads a miss as the guide being "promotional, not operational". The definition changing within one call fits that. One fact on the other side: castings capacity of 24,600 MT is about 6,150 MT a quarter. 4,900 MT produced would be about 80% foundry use, against the 52% sales-based figure (deck p.6, on file). My arithmetic, not filed. If the production figure is real, the foundry is not idle.

### Net-debt row: the payables stretch is now policy

The 23-Oct review asked whether the ₹231.5 Cr payables rise is usance LCs or acceptances, which would be debt-like (Notion, Q2 review).

- Answer: "Yes, it is here to sustain. We will be looking to work more on the trade payables side rather than, leverage our cash positions to buy materials going forward, as we are going into a capex cycle over the next 4 years" (p.7).
- What was not answered: the instrument. LC or open credit is still not disclosed.

What it does to the row. The bear reading on file was "the H1 release was timing" (Notion, Q2 review, separating test). Management now says the opposite: the payables stay. That removes the timing reading but strengthens the financing reading. Suppliers are being used to fund a four-year capex cycle. If the payables are LC-backed, net debt of ₹538.7 Cr ex-leases understates leverage by up to ₹231.5 Cr [INFERENCE, on file]. Please rule on whether the row counts them.

### Capex funding: the call contradicts the filing

- Filed: ₹450 Cr funded by "internal accruals and debt" (Notion, Q2 review, item 65).
- On the call: "Practically, it should be funded entirely from internal accruals" through FY31 (p.7). Debt is kept for demand arriving early (p.10).
- Split: ₹125 Cr is land and building to consolidate the Bangalore plants, which came with the Bagadia Chaitra acquisition (p.8). ₹325 Cr is capacity "including Aurangabad". Of the ₹740 Cr total, ₹250-260 Cr is committed; the rest is "modular" (pp.11, 16). Year one is ₹250-300 Cr, then ₹125-150 Cr a year (p.12).
- This confirms the 23-Oct inference: the ₹125 Cr part is relocation of acquired capacity, not growth.
- Asset productivity check. FY31 revenue potential is about ₹3,800 Cr (p.10), against the ₹2,350-2,400 Cr FY27 run-rate (p.20). That is about ₹1,425 Cr more, close to the ₹1,480 Cr the 23-Oct review said ₹740 Cr needs at 2.0x asset turns. My arithmetic, not filed. The ambition is consistent with today's turns. It is not a step-up.

An inconsistency on H1 capex. The MD said: "We didn't do INR250 crores in H1... about INR140 crores would have been the cash flow on capex" (p.21). The filed deck cash flow shows capex of ₹256.8 Cr (deck p.11, on file in the 23-Oct review). The gap is about ₹117 Cr. The net-debt bridge (₹552.1 Cr at March to ₹538.7 Cr at September, with ₹66 Cr of H1 free cash on the filed figure) fits the filed ₹256.8 Cr, not ₹140 Cr [INFERENCE]. One question to IR.

### Export row: total railway confirmed, export railway still missing

- Traction motor and railway: "Out of INR580 crores of revenue, about 27% is about INR156 crores... last year, it's about 32%" (p.18).
- Against Q2 FY26 (32% of ₹499 Cr total income, p.4) of about ₹160 Cr, railway is down about 2%. Against the Q1 derived ₹148 Cr on the row, it is up about 5% QoQ. My arithmetic, not filed.
- Export railway: not disclosed.
- New guidance: "overall exports will remain stable or slightly downward as a percentage of overall sales". Customers prefer to make the product in India, and Pitti "cannot... quantify" deemed exports (p.15).

The row still depends on reading. On Reading A (total exports ₹154.3 Cr) it passes. On Reading B (export railway) it cannot be tested, and management has now said the direct-export number will understate the business. That makes Reading B less testable over time. The ruling from 23-Oct is still needed.

### The subsidy question from 9-Oct is answered

The 9-Oct review asked if any FY27 subsidy is linked to Maharashtra state GST (Notion, 09-Oct review).

- The Package Scheme of Incentives is for the Aurangabad investment. Recovery is limited "based on the local Maharashtra sales" (pp.4, 6). That fits an SGST-linked incentive [INFERENCE on mechanism].
- Eligible up to ₹400 Cr. The scheme was sanctioned for 7 years, about ₹57 Cr a year. The company applied for 9 years, about ₹44 Cr a year, and is asking for 9 again (pp.6, 9).
- Current sales support only about ₹35-36 Cr a year. So the **whole FY27 claim is deferred to FY28** (p.9), when about ₹40 Cr a year is expected.
- About ₹70 Cr of past incentive is "in the process for disbursement". The figure is the analyst's; the MD did not confirm it (p.18).

What it touches:

- FY27 PAT. Nil subsidy income in FY27 confirms your own ₹135-150 Cr build over the brokers' ₹156-176 Cr (Notion, FY27E block).
- FY28 PAT. About ₹40 Cr pre-tax is about ₹30 Cr after tax at 25%, which is 13-17% of the broker FY28 range of ₹178-237 Cr. My arithmetic, not filed. FY28 growth will partly be a subsidy-timing effect again.
- The GST search. It was at Aurangabad (Notion, Q2 review), the same unit whose state-GST-linked incentive was just deferred. The deferral has an economic rationale on the call: don't start a 7-year clock you can't fill. Nobody asked about the search, and the transcript is silent. Two readings: the deferral is pure optimisation, or claims at that unit are under scrutiny [INFERENCE]. Test: any show-cause notice or demand before the FY28 claim is booked.

### Smaller items, tested

- EBITDA guide ₹370 Cr: "pretty much on track. If you look at the current quarter and simply extrapolate this, you should be getting there" (p.14). Extrapolating gives ₹180.1 Cr plus 2 x ₹91.3 Cr = about ₹363 Cr, a little under ₹370 Cr. My arithmetic, not filed.
- Revenue: ₹2,350-2,400 Cr "based on Q3 pricing" for 82,000 MT of laminations and 17,000 MT of castings (p.20). That is above the ₹2,300 Cr on file. H2 would need ₹621-646 Cr a quarter, 7-12% above Q2. My arithmetic.
- Margin: Q2 forex loss ₹4.61 Cr, booked above EBITDA (p.5). Adding it back, the margin is about 16.6% against 15.8% reported. Fuel pass-through from January. Employee cost is up QoQ for new capacity (p.14). "17% would be pretty much a very comfortable number" long term (p.11). The FY28 17-17.2% line on file is still management's line.
- Data centres: customers named are Cummins, Marathon and Nidec (p.4). The data-centre share is not expected to exceed "probably 9-odd percent" of revenue (p.13). That caps the strongest demand pillar on file (Notion, Verticals 5). Caterpillar is not named among data-centre customers. Its "commercial production from Q3 FY27" on file was not addressed.
- Customer concentration: the top five to seven customers stay the majority, grouped by beneficial owner. The example given was Progress Rail under Caterpillar (p.12).
- Scrap and by-product volume rose more than 60%. It is put down to higher HVA assemblies and a finished-goods overhang at half-year end (p.12). That partly explains the ₹83.8 Cr H1 inventory rise on file.
- Sloppiness: the transcript disclaimer says the audio was "uploaded on the stock exchange on 20th May 2026" (p.2). The audio was filed on 23-Oct (Notion, 24-Oct review). It is a template carry-over, like the "Q1FY27" deck cover on file. Not a flag.

### Two readings

The bull reading has evidence:

- EBITDA, revenue and lamination volume guides are held or raised.
- The capex is mostly modular and phased against orders, with only ₹250-260 Cr committed.
- The foundry may be about 80% used on a production basis.
- The FY28 subsidy restarts at about ₹40 Cr.
- Asset turns on the FY31 plan are consistent with today's.

The bear reading has the same weight:

- The castings guide was redefined after it missed, on a basis not in any filed table.
- The payables stretch is now stated policy to fund capex, and its instrument is still undisclosed.
- The capex funding description changed between the filing and the call.
- The MD's H1 capex figure is about ₹117 Cr below the filed cash flow.
- Data centres are capped at about 9%.
- Direct exports are guided to shrink as a share of sales.

The single observation that separates them: the Q3 FY27 print, about late January 2027 (net-debt row Next Check 25-Jan-2027). Two parts:

- Does net debt ex-leases fall to ₹491 Cr or below while payables hold near ₹475 Cr?
- Does the promised production-basis castings table show H1 plus Q3 at 12,750 MT or more, the three-quarter share of 17,000 MT?

If both pass, the cash and capacity story is real. If net debt stays above ₹539 Cr with payables stretched, the capex is outrunning cash even with suppliers funding it. My thresholds, not filed.

### Price and verdict

₹860 (screener.in, 28-Oct-2026 06:12 IST). 52-week range ₹675-1,228. Market cap ₹3,237 Cr. Notion carries ₹901 (screener.in, 24-Oct-2026). Down 4.6% since then and 9.4% since ₹949 on 23-Oct. My arithmetic, not filed:

- About 21.6-24.0x your own FY27 PAT build of ₹135-150 Cr.
- About 10.2x FY27 EBITDA of ₹370 Cr on enterprise value (₹3,237 Cr plus ₹538.7 Cr net debt).

There are no Entry, MoS, stop, trim or exit lines on file to test against.

The net-debt and castings falsifiers that fired on 22-Oct stay fired as written. The call adds a definitional dispute on castings and a policy statement on payables. The export row is still untestable on Reading B. There is no thesis to break, only a placeholder. On your rules I read this as no reason to start Role 1 before the Q3 print. WATCHLIST placeholder unchanged. That is your call.

## SKM Egg Prod.: price held, volume fell, and a new ₹180 Cr capex

SKM is in COMPANIES MASTER at WATCHLIST / AVOID. Promoter Verdict CONCERN. Entry Price Max and hard no-buy ceiling ₹160, MoS ₹130 (Notion, properties and Entry Zone). Three tracker rows are linked and were read: scheme brand and premium, Chairman Emeritus pay, authorised capital headroom.

The item is the transcript of the 23-Oct call, filed 27-Oct at 13:33 IST, two working days after the call (SKM_Egg_Prod_Submission_Of_Con_Call_Transcript_3d2646.txt, p.1). The 25-Oct review said this transcript was the document to test (Notion, Key Notes 25-Oct). It answers three open questions, opens two new ones, and leaves the cash questions untouched.

### 1. Volume and price, disclosed for the first time [MGMT]

The CFO gave powder sales volume (p.13):

- Q2 FY27 1,625 MT. Q1 1,831 MT. H1 3,457 MT, against 4,289 MT in H1 FY26.
- My arithmetic, not filed: Q2 is down 11.2% QoQ. H1 is down 19.4% YoY. FY26 was 7,126 MT (Notion, concall findings), so H2 FY26 was about 2,837 MT. H1 FY27 annualised is about 6,914 MT, 3% below FY26.

The MD gave average realisation "about 700 rupees" for the quarter, mix-dependent (p.6). He expects "700 plus" in H2 and "no very big correction" in the next six months (pp.6, 20).

Test against the checklist line "powder ASP below ₹650/kg means the cycle decisively rolled" (Notion, Quantitative monitoring checklist): **does not fire.** ₹700 is 3% below the FY26 ₹722/kg on file. The 24-Oct review recorded this leg as untestable; it is now tested, and it passes on management's word.

My arithmetic, not filed: 1,625 MT x ₹700/kg is about ₹114 Cr, 62% of Q2 standalone revenue of ₹184.15 Cr (Notion, 24-Oct). On FY26 the same sum gave about 67%. Q1 at the same price gives about ₹128 Cr of ₹179.20 Cr. So non-powder revenue rose from about ₹51 Cr to about ₹70 Cr QoQ while powder fell [INFERENCE: assumes the same ₹700 both quarters].

### 2. What management says broke the margin [MGMT]

- CFO: EBITDA fell from ₹38 Cr to ₹28 Cr QoQ "because of increase in feed raw material cost" (p.4).
- MD: the cost cannot be passed on. "International prices are decided by international competition" (p.7, p.8). The fix is importing soya, about 10% cheaper, six months of stock, "in another week or two" (pp.11, 15).
- MD on the US: "the US is able to offer cheaper", and "we have seen this impact in Japan that we had to reduce our prices" (p.6). An analyst called it "oversupply in the US market on account of excessive egg production" (p.5).

This changes how the 24-Oct read should be stated. The file's bear track was "cycle rolls FY27" on a bird-flu price collapse (Notion, Entry Zone; 24-Oct review). On the call, price is roughly held. The margin fall is volume (down 19%) plus feed cost. But the mechanism the file predicted is visible: US flocks repopulating ("two years' time to repopulate", Notion, concall) and US supply undercutting Japan, a premium market on file.

Two readings:

- Bull. Realisation is near ₹700, above the ₹650 line. The squeeze is feed cost, which the soya import and the own-farm project address. Volume can recover; South Korea approved SKM with two customers and a first order is expected October-December (p.9) [MGMT].
- Bear. Volume fell 19% in H1 at a steady price. That is customers buying less, not SKM choosing price over volume. The first price cut is already in Japan. MD's own 26-Jun warning, "if one ingredient goes up too much then they switch... this demand is gone forever" (Notion, concall), fits a volume fall at held prices.
- The observation that separates them: Q3 FY27 powder volume and ASP, due about February 2027. Volume back at or above 1,800 MT with ASP at or above ₹700 and standalone OPM at or above 20% backs the bull. Volume at or below 1,625 MT, or ASP below ₹650, or OPM at or below 16% confirms the rollover. The OPM lines are already on file (Notion, 24-Oct).

### 3. "₹100 Cr plus this year" against the filed run-rate

The MD said twice: "we are very confident of repeating 100 plus this year" and "we will do 100 crores plus for sure" (pp.7, 14) [MGMT]. The context is profit.

My arithmetic, not filed:

- H1 consolidated PAT is ₹41.61 Cr (Notion, 24-Oct). ₹100 Cr needs H2 of ₹58.4 Cr, about ₹29.2 Cr a quarter.
- That is 1.64x Q2's ₹17.78 Cr and above Q1's ₹23.82 Cr. It equals FY26's H2 (about ₹62 Cr), which ran on 23-26% OPM (Notion, sequential trajectory).
- In the same call he said the next six months would be "a continuity of the past six months with a small margin improvement" (p.6). H1 repeated gives about ₹83 Cr. The two statements do not reconcile.

Fair value consequence (my arithmetic, 5.266 Cr shares, Notion 24-Oct; destination 11-13x, Notion three-pillar):

| Earnings basis | PAT (₹ Cr) | EPS (₹) | Value at 11-13x (₹) |
|---|---|---|---|
| MD's "100 plus" | 100 | 19.0 | 209-247 |
| H1 annualised | 83.2 | 15.8 | 174-205 |
| Q2 annualised (on file 24-Oct) | 71.1 | 13.48 | 148-175 |
| Normalised track on file | 55-65 | 10-12 | 110-132 |

The pending ruling on re-weighting the three tracks (Notion, 24-Oct) now has a management number pulling the other way. A guide this far above the run-rate is itself a test of credibility: Q3 consolidated PAT at or above ₹29 Cr keeps it alive; Q3 at or below ₹20 Cr makes it a miss before Q4.

### 4. New: powder capacity 7,000 to 10,000+ MT for ₹180 Cr [MGMT]

- "Expanding our capacity from the current 7,000 tons to 10,000 plus tons with an overall investment of 180 crores", in effect "maybe by second half of next year", with "real sales increment" from FY28-29 (p.12).
- Funding: ₹30 Cr margin plus ₹150 Cr term loan at 8.25%. No interest subvention; the 3% subvention covers only the farm (p.19).
- Filling the capacity will take "three to five years" (p.19).

What it touches:

- Monitoring gate 10, "Powder capacity expansion announcement, 7,320 to 10,000 MT detailed plan and capex, FY27-28" (Notion, Monitoring Plan). It arrives on a call. I found no Reg 30 filing of a board approval for it in the filings indexed for SKM since 13-Oct (only results, the investor-meet outcome and this transcript). The analyst said "I heard that I think we've approved" (p.19). If a ₹180 Cr capex, about 18% of the ₹1,024 Cr market cap, was board-approved and not filed, that is a disclosure gap [INFERENCE on the rule]. Please check whether it was approved earlier, for example at the AGM.
- The ₹400 Cr programme on file was framed by the MD as "not a top line growth investment. It is a bottom line" (Notion, capex). This one is top line, and it is being added in the half when powder volume fell 19%.
- Unit economics, my arithmetic: interest on ₹150 Cr at 8.25% is about ₹12.4 Cr a year. 3,000 MT more at ₹700/kg is about ₹210 Cr of revenue at full fill, three to five years after a H2 FY28 start, so about FY31-33. The file already found the farm capex does not pay for itself on egg savings alone (₹9.7 Cr saving vs ₹13.6 Cr interest; Notion, unit economics). This adds a second interest line before the first project earns.
- Debt: the MD said "we totally about 450 crore debt we will have" (p.15). The file has about ₹420 Cr at peak (₹118 Cr plus ₹302 Cr; Notion, 14-Oct). Adding ₹150 Cr gives about ₹570 Cr (my arithmetic). Either the ₹450 Cr excludes the new loan, or part of the ₹302 Cr is not needed. Not stated. Both fit under the ₹1,000 Cr borrowing ceiling from the AGM, which now has a partial explanation.

### 5. Term loan, farm project and treasury [MGMT]

- The board approved a term loan of "nearly 300 crores, 200 from State Bank, 100 crores from Axis Bank", documents to be signed "next week" (pp.10-11). This confirms the 24-Oct observation that the ₹302 Cr loan was not drawn.
- The second-phase farm (20 lakh birds; 24 lakh in total) completes by September 2028 (pp.8, 10, 16). That matches FY28-29 commissioning on file. First rearing house in two weeks, chicks from 15-Nov, about one week late (p.10).
- The CFO keeps "a minimum of around 100 crores" cash. The MD said the finance division "has a target to go beyond 10%" on surplus funds, financed by borrowing at about 5.25% (p.18). Borrowing subsidised money to earn 10% or more on a treasury book needs risk-bearing assets [INFERENCE]. It is not said what they are. The ₹87.98 Cr of unexplained other current assets (Notion, 24-Oct) was not asked about.

### 6. Governance items inside the call

- **Goodwill ₹17.46 Cr.** Asked directly (p.13), the CFO said: "it's not a good will, it's a purchase cost of shares". The balance sheet labels it goodwill on consolidation (Notion, 24-Oct). No brand, no split, no accounting note. Tracker "SKM Universal scheme: brand + premium accounting": the falsifier is "premium left unexplained". The company was asked and did not explain it. I read this as Threatens.
- **Merger timing.** "By April next year... universal company will cease to exist" (p.12); "from 1-4-27 onwards" (p.13). Monitoring gate 7 had FY27 H1 (Notion); it now reads FY28 start.
- **Results timing (the 25-Oct check).** A shareholder said the results appeared on the website "only around 1230, 1240" while the call was set for 12:30 (p.9). The MD said he "had to press this in the morning itself" (p.20). So results and call were near-simultaneous. The exchange timestamp is still the clean test.
- **Slides shown on the call, not filed.** The CFO shared "some of the slides what we shared in our meeting" with a results snapshot (pp.3-4). No deck is in the filings indexed for SKM. Presentations to analysts or investors are meant to be filed with the exchanges before or with the meeting [INFERENCE on the rule]. The MD promised an investor presentation with results "next quarter onwards" (p.20). That is testable at Q3.
- **Opening remarks.** The CFO opened the earnings call with a page praising the CMD's Harvard programmes (p.3). This repeats the board outcome (Notion, 24-Oct). Who paid is still not stated.
- **Chairman Emeritus.** A shareholder congratulated SKM Maeilanandhan on a Padma Bhushan (p.15). Nothing on his pay. Tracker row unchanged.
- **Dividend.** "We will try to maintain the 25% even during the high investment phase" (p.15). 25% of ₹5 face value is ₹1.25, the FY26 rate. My arithmetic: about ₹6.6 Cr, a payout of 6.6% on ₹100 Cr PAT or 7.9% on ₹83 Cr. Above the 5% line under either reading of ruling 19. He also floated holding dividends lower "for the next two, three years" before settling on 25%.
- Internal slip: the farm trial is "400,000 layers" and then "100,000 birds" on the same page (p.16).

### Gate tests, leg by leg

- Gate 2 (disclosure quality: explain other current assets, give a ROCE for the ₹400 Cr capex) (Notion, Monitoring Plan): **fails on content.** Neither was asked or answered. Partly offset: volume and ASP are now on a filed document, and a deck is promised. This settles pending ruling (1) of 25-Oct only if you take the "transcript that answers" reading; please confirm.
- Checklist "powder ASP below ₹650/kg": **does not fire** (about ₹700).
- Checklist "other current assets above ₹40 Cr without disclosure": **still fired**; silent on the call.
- Risk 3 "CFO/PAT below 0.55x in FY27": **still fired** at 0.37x (Notion, 24-Oct); silent on the call.
- Gate 10 (powder expansion announced): **met on management's word**, not filed.
- Gate 7 (SKM Universal merger completion): **slipped** from FY27 H1 to about April 2027.
- Entry trigger, five legs: price fails at ₹195; Q1 OPM declining passes; AR other current assets, MD pay and Russia legs are still not logged or tested. One of five.

### Price

₹195 (screener.in, 28-Oct-2026 06:13 IST). 52-week ₹143-372, market cap ₹1,024 Cr. My arithmetic, not filed:

- Up 4.8% from ₹186 on file (Notion, 25-Oct).
- 21.9% above the ₹160 ceiling, ₹65 above the ₹130 MoS.
- 14.5x Q2 annualised EPS (₹13.48), 12.3x H1 annualised (₹15.8), 10.3x on the MD's ₹100 Cr (₹19.0).

### Verdict

No thesis-broken condition is listed on the page as such. The call weakens the "price collapse" form of the bear case, since ASP held near ₹700. It strengthens the volume and cost form: H1 powder volume down 19%, Japan prices cut on US supply, feed costs not passable. It adds a ₹180 Cr debt-funded capacity expansion at that moment, and a ₹100 Cr profit claim the H1 run-rate does not support. Governance moved slightly the wrong way: the goodwill premium was asked about and not explained, and results slides were shown but not filed. Price is still above the ₹160 line. WATCHLIST / AVOID stands. The track re-weighting ruling now has two inputs pulling in opposite directions. That is your call.

## Sterlite Tech.: filed transcript hedges the 30% line; price leaves the band

Sterlite is in COMPANIES MASTER as AVOID. The body says the position was exited near ₹600 in Jun-2026. The properties still show Position Size "Small (2-3%)" and Avg Cost ₹195; ruling R5 on that is still open (Notion, Q2 review, Rulings). Re-entry zone ₹178-222, MoS ₹178, which the 24-Oct review proposed to suspend pending a Role 1 refresh (indicative band ₹680-815) (Notion, Q2 review Step 8). The 24-Oct review read the call from an "auto-generated transcript, names garbled" (Notion, Q2 review Source). This is the company's filed transcript of the same 23-Oct call (Sterlite_Tech_..._c677ed.pdf, p.1, filed 27-Oct).

### What the filed record confirms

- The USD 1.2bn deal is "with another global hyperscaler" and "builds on the $1.11 billion product award letter... from another hyperscaler" (p.5). Additive, as logged. No name. Monitor #9 stays RED.
- Book ₹35,274 Cr, "up 4.6x from INR7,687 crores at the start of this financial year"; ₹3,021 Cr scheduled for Q3; spot "negligible" (p.7). The opening-book question (₹7,687 Cr vs ₹7,309 Cr logged) stands.
- Prysmian: not mentioned anywhere in the 16 pages. No analyst asked and management did not raise it.
- Utilisation: "we've not been sharing specific numbers" (p.13). Price: "we don't comment on prices specifically" (p.9). Third call running.
- Receivables: asked by Rabindra Nath Nayak (p.10). Not answered. The answer covered the refund, Speedon and finance cost only.
- Impairment: the analyst cites "INR49 crores"; the filed P&L line is ₹39 Cr (Notion, Q2 review Step 1). Only the ₹22 Cr Speedon PPE charge was explained (p.10). The ₹39 Cr credit charge is still unexplained on the filed record.
- Ex-LTSA Q2 orders: the analyst says "a very strong INR7,000 crores plus" (p.12), and management did not dispute it. My arithmetic, not filed: Q2 intake ₹18,926 Cr less the LTSA's ₹11,500 Cr is about ₹7,426 Cr. Less the LSA (₹2,700 Cr), the NA telco deal (₹2,000 Cr) and the India telco deal (₹960 Cr), that leaves about ₹1,766 Cr of regular flow, against about ₹3,000 Cr in Q1 (Notion, 24-Oct entry). The "thinner regular intake" inference is not contradicted.

### What the filed record corrects

1. The margin line belongs to the MD, and it is hedged. Notion logs it as the CFO's: "CFO: 'we would definitely like to perform or better what we have reported in Q2...'" (Notion, Role 5 Step 4). In the filed transcript, Ankit Agarwal (MD) says it. The wording is: "the target of Lakshya aligns with the target of what we plan to try to achieve from Q3 onwards" (p.11). Pressed again, he says results "in alignment with our project Lakshya, wherein we have already announced to increase it to 30%" (p.12). That gives two readings:
   - Firm floor: "perform or better what we have reported in Q2". That is 27.1% operational EBITDA on the company's basis, which includes other income; 25.7% ex-OI (Notion, Q2 review Step 1).
   - Aspiration: about 30% "from Q3", with "try" in the sentence.
   The 8C bull line (27% or more ex-OI on ₹3,000 Cr or more) tests something tighter than the firm floor. Please rule whether the Expectation Ledger row "30% operating margin from Q3" (proposed p 0.50) is graded on 30% or on the Q2 floor.
2. Speaker attribution. In the filed transcript every Q&A answer comes from the MD, including payables, finance cost, the refund and working capital (p.10-13). The CFO only presents the financials (p.6-8). Notion says "CFO took finance and WC answers" (Role 5 Step 0). The promoter-group MD owns all the forward statements.
3. Net zero. The filed transcript says "net zero by 2030" (p.8). The "2050 (call) vs 2030 (deck)" contradiction logged in Role 5 Step 1 was an auto-transcript error. Drop it.
4. DC share. The CFO says Data Center was "33% in H1 FY'27" (p.7). The analyst puts Q2 at "40% to 42%" (p.13), and the MD says "close to around 50%" for the year (p.14). The 24-Oct Claude Code entry read the deck chart as DC & Cloud 53%, Enterprise 14%, Telecom 33% (Notion, 24-Oct entry, thesis-broken triggers). That reading looks like a label swap. It does not move any trigger: Enterprise + DC under 22% is far away on either reading.

One claim is new on the record. The CFO says backlog-led revenue gives "more stable realization through the cycle" (p.7). But the LTSAs value at "prevailing selling prices" (Notion, 04-Oct), and management refuses to comment on price. This is ruling R1 in a sentence: management asserts price stability and gives no number that would test it.

### Prysmian silence: how to count it

Ruling R4 asks whether two silent calls downgrade (page note) or three evasions are needed (protocol). The filed record adds one fact: nobody asked. Two readings:
- Protocol reading: an evasion needs a question. Here there is none, so the count does not move.
- Page-note reading: a live emphasis of matter worth about ₹970 Cr at full loss (Notion, Correction C4), not raised by management on two results calls, is concealment whether or not someone asked.
Please rule. It decides whether the Grade B on credibility holds.

### Price and the hurdle

₹911 (screener.in, 28-Oct-2026 06:13 IST). 52-week range ₹84.6-1,054, market cap ₹46,825 Cr. My arithmetic, not filed:
- Up 10.2% from ₹827 on file (24-Oct).
- 32.8x the A21 run-rate diluted EPS of ₹27.8 (Notion, Q2 review Step 6.5), above the 30x Telecom Equipment cap.
- Using the page's formula, Hurdle Ratio = 1.908 x (cap / current PE). At 30x it is about 1.75; at 28x about 1.63; at 25x about 1.46. All are below 1.953. On 24-Oct, at about ₹813, the same formula gave 1.96, "PASS by a hair" at 30x (Notion, Q2 review Step 7). That pass is gone at this price.
- 11.8% above the top of the indicative ₹680-815 band. T1 at 28-30x (₹778-834) is now 85-92% of the price, so the residual is positive again.
- If the about-30% margin lands in Q3 on about ₹3,000 Cr, run-rate EPS would be about ₹33-35 (Notion, Section E inference). At ₹911 that is about 26-28x. The price now needs the MD's "try to achieve from Q3" to land.
- 4.1-5.1x the ₹178-222 zone on file.

### Verdict

No thesis-broken trigger fires. The transcript moves no gate: margin passed, cash failed, LTSA additive, counterparty unnamed. It hedges the boldest forward line and corrects four items in the record. The price change matters more for your pending rulings than the filing does. At ₹911 the Hurdle Ratio no longer passes even at the 30x cap. R3 (30x vs 25x) now decides between CONDITIONAL and CONDITIONAL-low, not between PASS and CONDITIONAL. Exited / AVOID stays where it is unless you rule otherwise. That is your call.

## Embassy Develop: 71% of warrants converted in 15 days, so the bear leg cannot fire

Embassy is in COMPANIES MASTER as WATCHLIST / AVOID. The position has been EXITED since the pledge trigger fired in June. Entry Price Max is ₹49 and MoS is ₹40 (Notion, properties). Avg Cost ₹68 is still on the property line from the old holding, but Position Size reads "None (exited)". All five linked tracker rows were read. Four items: a second warrant conversion (item 31), the insider-trade pair that covers both conversions (items 28 and 29), and the Q2 board date (item 30).

What item 31 says [FILED] (Embassy_Develop_Announcement_under_Regulation_30_LODR-Al_14df8c.pdf):
- On 27-Oct-2026 Embassy Property Developments Pvt Ltd (EPDPL) converted 1,22,00,000 more warrants at ₹111.51. It paid the 75% balance of ₹1,02,03,16,500, about ₹102.03 Cr (p.1).
- Paid-up capital is now 1,41,38,33,433 shares. The promoter group goes from 43.13% to 43.62% (p.2).
- EPDPL goes from 20,47,92,592 shares (14.61%) to 21,69,92,592 (15.35%) (p.5).
- 93,18,900 warrants remain. They must convert "on or before April 11, 2027" (p.6).

Items 28 and 29 have no PDF. The titles are the disclosure. They tie exactly to the two conversions added together (my arithmetic, not filed):
- 1.10 Cr (23-Oct) + 1.22 Cr (27-Oct) = 2.32 Cr shares. This matches the 23,200,000 in both titles.
- 2.32 Cr x ₹111.51 = ₹258.70 Cr. This matches "equity worth Rs 25870.32 lacs".
- ₹6,467.58 lakh / 2.32 Cr = ₹27.88 a warrant. That is 25% of ₹111.51, the upfront paid on 12-Oct. So the "disposal" of warrants is the conversion itself, not a sale to anyone else [INFERENCE from the tie]. The full PIT form would add the dates and the holder's pre and post figures. Nothing in the titles conflicts with item 31.

Internal checks pass. 1,40,16,33,433 + 1,22,00,000 = 1,41,38,33,433. The promoter group's pre-allotment 43.13% (60.45 Cr shares) plus 1.22 Cr gives 61.67 Cr / 141.38 Cr = 43.62%. EPDPL 21.70 / 141.38 = 15.35%. All recompute (my arithmetic).

### Tested against the page

The arithmetic (my arithmetic, not filed):
- Converted so far: 2.32 Cr of 3,25,18,900 warrants = 71.3%, done 15 days after the 12-Oct allotment.
- Cash received on the issue: ₹90.65 Cr upfront + ₹92.00 Cr (23-Oct) + ₹102.03 Cr (27-Oct) = ₹284.68 Cr of ₹362.62 Cr. Still due: 93,18,900 x ₹83.63 = ₹77.94 Cr.
- These 1.22 Cr shares cost ₹136.04 Cr in all. At ₹48.8 they are worth ₹59.54 Cr, so EPDPL paid about ₹76.5 Cr above market value. Across both tranches, 2.32 Cr shares cost ₹258.70 Cr against a market value of ₹113.2 Cr.

Warrant tracker row (falsifier: "Lapse, or part-exercise below 50% of the warrants, by about 12-Apr-2027", Tracker). Leg by leg:
- Bear leg (below 50% by the deadline): can no longer fire. 71.3% is already converted.
- Bull leg ("full exercise below ₹83.6"): 71.3% done, both tranches at about ₹48 to ₹49, far below the ₹83.6 break-even logged on 13-Oct. Not complete. 93,18,900 warrants are left.
- Date fix still open: the filing says 11-Apr-2027. The row says "about 12-Apr-2027". The 24-Oct save deferred this correction, and it is still not written.

Insider-buying tracker row (falsifier: no follow-on buy and no warrant conversion "on the self-imposed 6-month timetable", Tracker). The conversion leg is now mostly done, and it is inside the window. The pending ruling (does a warrant conversion count as the follow-on that retires the "insider non-buying" signal?) now covers ₹194 Cr of balance payments, not ₹92 Cr.

Re-entry gates. Your rule is that all three fire together.
- Gate #1, pledge below 25% of total. The page body still carries 26.95% (15-Jul event log), which is 37.47 Cr pledged shares. On today's count that is 37.47 / 141.38 = 26.50%. About 2.12 Cr shares would need releasing (my arithmetic). The tracker's Sep SHP reading [SECONDARY, MarketsMojo/Screener] was 57.48% of a 42.68% promoter holding, which is 24.53% of total, or about 34.11 Cr shares. On today's count that is 24.13% (my arithmetic). So the gate depends on the reading: it fails on the page figure and passes on the secondary figure. The ruling from 24-Oct is still needed. The filed SHP encumbrance table is still not read.
- Gate #2, presales tracking the ₹6,000 Cr own floor: not touched. It still waits on the rolling-vs-fixed ruling.
- Gate #3, cost of debt toward 10%: not touched. It is logged at about 14% (Notion, concall). Direction only: if the ₹102 Cr retires promoter-group debt that management said accrues above 15% (Notion, concall Step 4C), that saves at least about ₹15 Cr a year. On the full ₹284.68 Cr received, about ₹43 Cr a year (my arithmetic at 15%). But Gate #3 is written on institutional cost of debt, and this filing gives no coupon. It does not fire.

Thesis-broken check: none fires.

### Two readings, same bar

Bull:
- ₹194 Cr of balance payments in five days, at 2.3x market, is a costly signal. It came fast, not at the deadline.
- It is the opposite of the 2024 pattern on file, when 4.75 Cr warrants lapsed and ₹132.49 Cr was forfeited (Notion, AR Step 6).
- The pledge reading moves the right way mechanically. Every unpledged new share dilutes the pledged share of total.

Bear:
- Management said on the call that the warrant is "not additional liquidity". The proceeds are earmarked to repay Embassy Group shareholder debt (Notion, concall Step 4C; AGM Step 6). If ₹284.68 Cr goes straight back to the same group, the company's cash does not change [INFERENCE]. Again, the filing says nothing about use.
- For the promoter group, swapping a debt claim on a cash-burning company for equity costs less than the "₹76.5 Cr above market" arithmetic suggests. That arithmetic assumes the claim was worth par [INFERENCE].
- Net institutional debt rose ₹369 Cr in Q2 to about ₹3,732 Cr (Tracker, net debt row). The warrant money does not touch that line if it only clears promoter debt.

The one observation that separates them: the Q2 results on 3-Nov-2026. If they show shareholder or related-party debt down by about the ₹284.68 Cr received, with net institutional debt not above ₹3,732 Cr, it is a clean deleveraging swap. If shareholder debt is flat, or institutional debt rises again, the conversion is a book entry inside the group.

### Item 30: the board date pulls the test forward

The board meets Tuesday 3-Nov-2026 for Q2 and H1 FY27 results. The trading window stays closed up to and including 5-Nov-2026 [FILED] (Embassy_Develop_Board_Meeting_Intimation_for_Considerati_48d671.pdf, p.1). Three tracker rows carry a next check of 14-Nov-2026, set as "mid-Nov, date estimated" (Tracker, presales row and insider-buying row). The real date is 11 days earlier. Those rows should move to 3-Nov.

What 3-Nov tests, pre-registered from the page:
- H1 CFO against the logged Q1 burn of minus ₹285 Cr. Q2 positive is the bull case; H1 at minus ₹570 Cr or worse is the bear case (Notion, Key Notes 09-Oct).
- Shareholder debt down by the warrant cash. This is the use-of-proceeds test above.
- The cost of debt figure for Gate #3, if it is disclosed.

Small error: the Screener summary for item 30 says "half-year FY26". The filing says the half year ended 30-Sep-2026, which is H1 FY27.

Timing note. Both conversions fall inside a closed trading window. The 24-Oct save covered this: the price is fixed well above market, so any information gap works against the promoter, not minority holders. Not a flag.

### What it does not say
- Use of the ₹102.03 Cr, or of the ₹284.68 Cr in total.
- Whether any of the money is pledge-funded or margin-funded. This was question 4 in the Q1 review and was evaded on the call (Notion, concall Step 3E).
- Why it was split into 1.10 Cr and 1.22 Cr four days apart, and not done in one tranche.

### Price and fair value

₹48.8 (screener.in, 28-Oct-2026 06:10 IST). 52-week ₹39.4 to ₹96.7, market cap ₹6,846 Cr. Against the lines on file (my arithmetic):
- 0.4% below the ₹49 Entry Price Max and 22% above the ₹40 MoS. Still inside the zone, up 0.8% from ₹48.4 on file (24-Oct).
- The ₹108 base target implies about 30.3% a year over three years, above the 25% hurdle.
- The exercise price is now 2.29x market.
- Fair value does not move. The ₹108 target rests on the 0.70x cash multiplier (Notion, AR Step 11), and a debt-to-equity swap inside the group does not change operating cash conversion. The 1.7% dilution so far is too small to move NAV per share materially.

Verdict: no thesis-broken trigger fires. The warrant row's bear leg is now dead, and the price stays inside the zone. Gate #1 still depends on which pledge figure you accept, Gate #2 still waits on your ruling, and Gate #3 has not fired. On your rule, the name stays EXITED, monitoring only. 3-Nov is now the date that decides whether this cash is real deleveraging. That is your call.
